*Chenbo Xia,Chao Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套可复现的流水线，将多数据集SMPLX人体动作重定向为Galaxea R1 Pro轮式人形机器人的可执行移动-操作行为，覆盖预处理、运动学重定向、规划层与强化学习策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-人形重定向研究多集中于腿式平台，支持由通用人体动作驱动的轮式人形"运动-操作耦合"系统较少。R1 Pro无腿关节（平面三轮底盘+躯干+双臂），人体下肢动作须重新分配到底盘运动与躯干姿态，同时保持操作相关的臂部几何，因而需要专门的流水线。

Method: 基于GMR可配置通用动作重定向与BeyondMimic物理仿真R1 Pro学习框架构建流水线：①标准体型预处理与平面底盘归一化；②形态感知差分逆运动学；③以肩为根的分层臂部重定向；④面向弯腰/下蹲的连续躯干替代；⑤参考扭转驱动的规划层，在迟滞、运动学连续性、加速度与执行器速率约束下将平面底盘运动解码为连续三轮转向与滚动指令；⑥在Isaac Lab中训练21维BaseDecode策略，采用方向性关节限位缩放、聚焦式上身跟踪与分阶段轮-地接触奖励。

Result: 系统实现了从人体动作数据到物理可跟踪的轮式人形移动-操作行为的完整桥接，而非仅可视化的重定向器。定量策略对比结果尚未给出，计划在后续修订中补充。

Conclusion: 该工作为轮式人形平台的人体动作重定向与物理可执行策略学习提供了可复现的端到端方案，弥补了腿式平台之外的空白；实证性量化评估有待后续工作完成。

Abstract: Human-to-humanoid retargeting has largely been studied on legged platforms, while comparatively few wheeled-humanoid systems support coupled locomotion and manipulation from general human motion. Building on GMR's configurable general-motion retargeting and BeyondMimic's physically simulated R1 Pro learning framework, we present a reproducible pipeline that converts multi-dataset SMPLX motion into executable loco-manipulation behavior for the Galaxea R1 Pro wheeled humanoid. The robot has a planar three-wheel base, a serial torso, and two arms but no leg joints, so human lower-body motion must be redistributed across base motion and torso posture without sacrificing manipulation-relevant arm geometry. Our pipeline combines canonical body-shape preprocessing, planar-base normalization, morphology-aware differential inverse kinematics, shoulder-rooted hierarchical arm retargeting, and continuous torso substitution for bending and squatting. A reference-twist-driven planning layer then decodes planar base motion into continuous three-wheel steering and rolling commands subject to hysteresis, kinematic continuity, acceleration, and actuator-rate limits. Finally, a 21-dimensional BaseDecode policy is trained in Isaac Lab with directional joint-limit scaling, focused upper-body tracking, and a staged wheel-contact reward. The resulting system provides a complete bridge from human motion data to physically trackable wheeled-humanoid loco-manipulation rather than a visualization-only retargeter; quantitative policy comparisons remain scheduled for a later revision.

</details>


### [25] [GeoTrussRover: Morphological Computation with Contact-Semantic Control Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11361)
*Muyuan Ma,Yi Zhang,Yang Yang,Xuanyan Zheng,Ruiqi Hu,Boxuan Ke,Zhenyu Chen,Yicong Lin,Xin Hao Yang,Daliang Xiao,Zhinan Hou,Wanhao Niu,Yuan Sun,Yan Yang,Yue Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GeoTrussRover系统，将电驱动变几何桁架（VGT）与轮式底盘结合，通过接触语义形态规划与控制，用四种接触语义基元刻画21根杆件的高维协调关系，实现低维表示下的可复用运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重构机器人能在固定构型无法通过障碍时改变接触几何，而变几何桁架（VGT）可将形状变化分配到承力结构中；但将其与移动底盘耦合会带来高维协调难题，亟需一种低维任务表示与高效复用方法。

Method: 1) 求解一次源穿越任务，提取四种接触语义基元，描述21根杆件间的协调关系；2) 采用物理约束投影，在保持相同接触拓扑的前提下将基元适配到未见台阶高度；3) 若所有阶段仍可行，则不重新计算完整运动，仅对违反新物理约束的单个阶段重算；4) 使用全空间QP跟踪适配后的运动，并校正杆件与轮子误差。

Result: 从0.10m向0.075m迁移时，目标函数评估次数较全量重算减少63.7%；接触阶段可行性分析覆盖0.10–0.46m台阶高度（1.08–4.97倍轮半径），上界接近理论可行边界；电驱动样机可穿越2.11倍轮半径。

Conclusion: 该方法以低维表示将任务协调信息存储于超冗余承力形态中，并在运动过程中复用，显著降低高维协调的规划开销，验证了接触语义基元迁移与分阶段重算的有效性。

Abstract: Reconfigurable robots can change their contact geometry when a fixed body cannot negotiate an obstacle. A variable-geometry truss (VGT) distributes this shape change through a load-bearing structure, but coupling it to a mobile base creates a high-dimensional coordination problem. GeoTrussRover combines an electrically actuated VGT, a wheeled base, and contact-semantic morphology planning and control. We solve one source traversal and extract four contact-semantic primitives that describe coordination among 21 members. Physics-constrained projection adapts them to unseen step heights with the same contact topology. When every phase remains feasible, adaptation does not recompute the complete motion. If one phase violates the new physical constraints, only that phase is recomputed. A full-space QP then tracks the adapted motion and corrects member and wheel errors. For transfer from 0.10m to 0.075m, the method reduces objective-function evaluations by 63.7% relative to full recomputation. Contact-phase feasibility analysis covers step heights from 0.10 to 0.46m, or 1.08 to 4.97 wheel radii, with the upper value near the theoretical feasible boundary. The electric prototype traverses 2.11 wheel radii. The resulting low-dimensional representation stores task coordination in a hyper-redundant, load-bearing morphology and reuses it during locomotion.

</details>


### [26] [SwarmNxt: Open-source Software-Hardware Platform for Fast and Agile Aerial Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11382)
*Charbel Toumieh,Niel Mistry,Benjamin Jarvis,Simon Jeger,Peize Liu,Shaojie Shen,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 SwarmNxt，一个基于开源 OmniNxt 硬件构建的开源软件平台，为自主无人机集群实验提供端到端工具链与 ROS 2 多智能体导航框架，并通过六机分散规划避碰和四机机载深度估计集群飞行两项实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人集群在安全、安保及搜救等时间敏感任务中具有变革潜力，但实现该潜力需要鲁棒的自主集群导航，这仍是活跃的研究难题。现有平台进一步制约了进展：商用无人机多为闭源，或缺乏敏捷、基于视觉的集群飞行所需的机载算力；同时在多台空中机器人上开发、部署与维护软件需要大量工程投入。

Method: 提出 SwarmNxt，一个构建于开源 OmniNxt 无人机硬件之上的开源软件平台，提供端到端工具链：包含详细硬件组装说明与视频教程、用于并行软件部署和集群级更新的自动化工具，以及基于 ROS 2 的自主导航框架。该平台将当前先进的感知、规划与控制集成于单一 ROS 2 多智能体系统中。验证实验为：六机集群执行分散式规划与高速机间避碰；四机集群在充满障碍的环境中执行带机载深度估计的集群飞行。

Result: 两项真实世界实验均在室内进行，全局位置来自外部动作捕捉系统；感知、规划与控制均机载运行。六机集群实现分散式规划与高速机间避碰，四机集群在障碍环境中完成机载深度估计的集群飞行。

Conclusion: SwarmNxt 为物理集群实验提供了开放的研究基础设施，降低了多机集群系统开发与部署的工程门槛，并通过真实飞行实验验证了其支持分散式规划、机间避碰与机载深度估计的能力。

Abstract: Aerial robot swarms have the potential to transform time-critical safety, security, and search-and-rescue operations. By coordinating multiple robots, they can rapidly survey disaster sites, map collapsed or GPS-denied environments, and search cluttered areas faster than a single robot, reducing response times and minimizing risks to first responders. Realizing this potential, however, requires robust autonomous swarm navigation, which remains an active research challenge. Progress is further constrained by existing platforms, as commercial drones are often closed-source or lack the onboard computational resources needed for agile, vision-based collective flight. Moreover, developing, deploying, and maintaining software across multiple aerial robots requires significant engineering effort. To address these challenges, we present SwarmNxt, an open-source software platform built on the open-source OmniNxt drone hardware. SwarmNxt provides an end-to-end toolkit, including detailed hardware assembly instructions with a video tutorial, automation tools for parallel software deployment and swarm-wide updates, and a ROS 2-based framework for autonomous navigation. The platform integrates state-of-the-art control, planning, and depth estimation into a single ROS 2 multi-agent system, providing an open research infrastructure for physical swarm experimentation. We validate SwarmNxt through two real-world experiments: a six-drone swarm performing decentralized planning with high-speed inter-drone collision avoidance, and a four-drone swarm executing collective flight with onboard depth estimation in an obstacle-filled environment. Both experiments were run indoors with global position from external motion capture; perception, planning, and control run onboard.

</details>


### [27] [Safety-aware Skill Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11433)
*A K M Nadimul Haque,Sheila Sutjipto,Marc G. Carmichael,Teresa Vidal-Calleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dist-GPRL，一种距离感知与安全引导的强化学习框架，用于结构化机器人技能自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于强化学习的技能自适应框架通常需要固定观测或严格控制的探索调度等限制性假设以维持稳定性。在杂乱和动态环境中，无限制探索可能导致不安全行为和学习不稳定，尤其是当任务相关观测位于障碍物附近或涉及移动物体时。

Method: 基于高斯过程（GP）技能参数化，顺序自适应稀疏轨迹途经点的重叠局部窗口而非在每个策略步修改完整技能；原始策略输出通过GP协方差结构相关联，产生时间一致的轨迹更新，同时降低动作空间与全局轨迹自适应的信用分配难度。安全通过两种互补引导实现：由Hausdorff Approximation Planner（HAP）导出的安全子空间先验，将策略探索偏向可行区域；动态更新的距离场间隙与梯度奖励提供局部障碍感知。轨迹运动学相似性正则化器在自适应过程中保持示教的速度与加速度特性。

Result: 在仿真中的两个动态物体操作任务上评估，并将学习到的策略迁移到真实机器人执行。实验结果显示，相比基线方法，任务成功率更高、碰撞频率更低、学习更稳定，同时保持示教技能的运动学特征。

Conclusion: Dist-GPRL通过结合GP技能参数化、安全子空间先验与距离场引导，实现了结构化机器人技能的安全稳定自适应，并具备良好的仿真到真实迁移能力。

Abstract: Skill adaptation frameworks based on reinforcement learning often require restrictive assumptions to maintain stability, such as fixed observations or tightly controlled exploration schedules. In cluttered and dynamic environments, however, unrestricted exploration can lead to unsafe behaviour and unstable learning, particularly when task-relevant observations lie near obstacles or involve moving objects. In this work, we present Dist-GPRL, a distance-aware and safety-guided reinforcement learning framework for structured robot skill adaptation. Building upon Gaussian Process (GP)-based skill parameterisation, our framework sequentially adapts overlapping local windows of sparse trajectory via-points rather than modifying the complete skill at every policy step. Raw policy outputs are correlated through the GP covariance structure, producing temporally coherent trajectory updates while reducing the action-space and credit-assignment difficulties associated with global trajectory adaptation. Safety is incorporated through two complementary forms of guidance. A safe-subspace prior derived from the Hausdorff Approximation Planner (HAP) biases policy exploration toward feasible regions, while dynamically updated distance field clearance and gradient rewards provide local obstacle awareness. A trajectory-kinematics similarity regulariser further preserves the demonstrated velocity and acceleration characteristics during adaptation. We evaluate the framework on two dynamic object-manipulation tasks in simulation and transfer the learned policy to real-world robot execution. Experimental results demonstrate higher task success, lower collision frequency, and more stable learning than the baselines, while preserving the kinematic characteristics of the demonstrated skill.

</details>


### [28] [FARM: Reading Failure Signals from the Internal Predictive States of a Frozen Robotic World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11445)
*Haoran Pei,Mingrui Luo,Senbao Wang,Haoran Lv,Jie Guo,Sheng Zhong,Ruixi Ci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出FARM，仅训练33,985参数的监督读出层，从冻结的VLA-JEPA世界模型预测状态中解码故障信息，实现因果、可迁移、低开销的机器人执行监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的机器人部署需要在线故障监控，但现有监控器主要依赖代理信号或需训练专用监控组件。作者探究冻结的预训练机器人世界模型内部预测状态是否已包含可直接解码的故障信息。

Method: FARM在冻结的VLA-JEPA预测状态上仅训练一个33,985参数的监督读出层，输出逐步故障分数和因果轨迹风险。在七个源任务上进行五折out-of-fold评估，并在10任务基准上与15个匹配基线对比；在PIPER X、SO-101、Franka四种真实机器人群体上测试固定读出迁移与仅读出适配。

Result: 七源任务五折评估达到85.68/88.59的池化AUROC/AUPRC；在10任务基准上Seen性能优于15个匹配基线；在真实机器人上实现无需更新预测主干的部署迁移；可区分故障与部分因果历史；冻结状态可用后平均CUDA延迟仅增加0.2256 ms。

Conclusion: 冻结的预测世界模型状态可作为可复用特征，用于因果、可迁移且低开销的执行监控。

Abstract: Reliable robot deployment requires online failure monitoring, yet existing monitors mainly derive risk from proxy signals or train dedicated monitoring components. We ask whether the internal predictive states of a frozen pretrained robotic world model already contain directly decodable failure information. Failure-Aware Readout from World Models (FARM) trains only a 33,985-parameter supervised readout over frozen VLA-JEPA predictive states, producing step-wise failure scores and causal trajectory risk. Five-fold out-of-fold evaluation across seven source tasks reaches 85.68/88.59 pooled AUROC/AUPRC, and FARM gives the best Seen performance among 15 matched baselines on the 10-task benchmark. Across four real-robot populations on PIPER X, SO-101, and Franka, fixed-readout transfer and readout-only adaptation test deployment shifts without updating the predictive backbone. FARM also discriminates failures from partial causal histories and adds 0.2256 ms mean CUDA latency once the frozen state is available. These results support frozen predictive world-model states as reusable features for causal, transferable, and low-overhead execution monitoring.

</details>


### [29] [3D Euler-Angle Orientation Control for Two-Ray Fading Mitigation in Maritime Air-to-Sea Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11476)
*Mohammed Bajja,Abdoul Karim A. H. Saliah,Hajar El Hammouti,Daniel Bonilla Licea,Giuseppe Silano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将无人机三维姿态作为物理层控制变量，通过天线相位中心位移调控海面双径干涉，提出闭式最小范数姿态解与可微软最小规则，并由约束NMPC在倾转多旋翼上跟踪实现，显著提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上空海链路受视距直射径与海面反射径的双径干涉影响，破坏性叠加会造成深度衰落。现有缓解方法主要优化无人机位置或轨迹，未利用姿态这一自由度。

Method: 将完整三维姿态建模为塑造双径干涉的物理层控制变量，利用天线相位中心位移改变两径相位关系。在小角度与远场假设下，把相长干涉条件简化为欧拉角上的仿射约束，得到闭式的最小范数姿态候选族；再用可微软最小值规则生成平滑参考，由约束非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）在全驱动倾转多旋翼上跟踪，并满足执行器限幅。

Result: 相比仅俯仰（pitch-only）基准，累计吞吐量提升11.4%；相比零姿态（zero-orientation）基线提升22.2%，同时保持轨迹跟踪性能并遵守执行器约束。

Conclusion: 三维姿态可作为有效物理层控制手段缓解海上双径衰落，所提闭式姿态解与NMPC方案在保证跟踪与执行器限制的前提下提升通信吞吐量。

Abstract: Maritime Air-to-Sea links are dominated by a line-of-sight ray and a sea-surface reflected ray whose destructive combination produces deep fades. Existing mitigation strategies optimize Unmanned Aerial Vehicle position or trajectory but leave attitude unexploited. This paper treats the full three dimensional attitude as a physical-layer control variable that shapes the two-ray interference through antenna phase-center displacement. Under small-angle and far-field assumptions, the constructive-interference condition reduces to an affine constraint in the Euler angles and admits a closed-form family of minimum-norm attitude candidates. A differentiable soft-minimum rule yields a smooth reference tracked by a constrained Nonlinear Model Predictive Control controller on a fully-actuated tilting multirotor. The proposed scheme increases cumulative throughput by 11.4% over a pitch-only benchmark and 22.2% over a zero-orientation baseline, while preserving trajectory tracking and respecting actuator limits.

</details>


### [30] [CARLAverse: A Highly Modular, Distributed, and Multimodal Framework for Human-in-the-Loop Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11478)
*Patrick Rebling,Philipp Nenninger,Reiner Kriesten*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARLAverse 是一个开源多模态 HITL 仿真生态系统，通过分布式物理架构将高频率触觉控制回路与网络瓶颈解耦，支持驾驶、骑行、行人多智能体在共享虚拟环境中的跨机构低延迟交互实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要涵盖弱势道路使用者（VRU）的混合交通场景测试，而纯人工代理难以复现真实的人类社会性协商行为。现有 HITL 多智能体平台受网络延迟与同步约束限制，无法提供高保真触觉反馈。

Method: 提出分布式物理架构：延迟敏感的自身车辆动力学与高频力反馈在客户端节点本地计算，中央 CARLA 服务器负责 NPC 物理与全局交通编排。基于模块化硬件抽象，将 DrivoCARLA（驾驶）、CycloCARLA（骑行）、WalkoCARLA（行人）集成到共享虚拟环境。

Result: 将触觉控制回路与网络瓶颈解耦，实现可扩展、跨机构的 HITL 实验，且不牺牲物理沉浸感；支持驾驶、骑行、行人多模态协同仿真。

Conclusion: CARLAverse 为混合交通中 VRU 交互研究提供了开源、可扩展的多模态仿真平台，代码与文档已公开于 https://git.ieem-ka.de/simulator-environments/carlaverse。

Abstract: The development of autonomous driving demands comprehensive testing in mixed-traffic scenarios involving vulnerable road users (VRUs), where purely artificial agents often fail to capture authentic human social negotiations. While human-in-the-loop (HITL) simulators enable safe investigation of these interactions, existing multi-agent platforms struggle with the network latency and synchronization constraints required for high-fidelity haptic feedback. To resolve this, we present CARLAverse, an open-source, multimodal simulation ecosystem. Extending modular hardware abstraction, CARLAverse integrates driving (DrivoCARLA), cycling (CycloCARLA), and pedestrian (WalkoCARLA) simulators into a shared virtual environment. Its core methodological contribution is a distributed physics architecture: latency-critical ego dynamics and high-frequency force feedback are computed locally on client nodes, while a central CARLA server orchestrates non-player character (NPC) physics and global traffic. By decoupling haptic control loops from network bottlenecks, CARLAverse enables scalable, cross-institutional HITL experiments without compromising physical immersion. Code and documentation: https://git.ieem-ka.de/simulator-environments/carlaverse

</details>


### [31] [Using Automated Vehicles Operational Data to Confirm Safety and Anticipate Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11549)
*Riccardo Donà,Espedito Rusciano,Germana Trentadue,Anastasios Tsakalidis,Maria Cristina Galassi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 欧盟新法规引入自动驾驶系统在役监测与报告（ISMR）数据采集条款，借鉴UNECE及核能、运输领域经验，通过收集真实世界数据支撑安全认证目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统型式认证验证方案难以覆盖自动驾驶系统安全评估中大量未披露的未知风险，因此需要建立真实世界数据采集机制来确认同源安全目标。

Method: 采用文本与制度分析，将欧盟法规中的ISMR规定与UNECE最佳实践（ISMR）以及核能生产、运输领域的运营数据采集监管方法进行对比，并阐述数据采集、场景目录构建与经验共享的机制。

Result: 确立ISMR框架：通过采集真实世界数据，使安全主管机构获得确认认证安全目标所需信息；将运行中发现的与安全相关的驾驶场景存入场景目录，用于研究其他ADS类型在类似交通冲突中的应对方式。

Conclusion: ISMR被视为应对ADS安全评估挑战的必要工具，其经验可在OEM与安全主管机构之间共享，弥补传统认证验证方案的不足。

Abstract: European Union (EU) policymakers adopted revolutionary data collection provisions for Automated Driving Systems (ADS) in the recently approved regulation that allows driverless vehicles to be operated on public roads. The framework is inspired by best practices developed at the United Nations Economic Commission for Europe(UNECE) level: the In-Service Monitoring and Reporting (ISMR); and by similar operational data collection regulatory approaches in nuclear energy production and transportation fields. The collection of real-world data will enable the competent safety authorities to gather the information needed to confirm the homologation safety target. Safety-relevant driving scenarios discovered during the real-world operation of a given ADS can also be stored in a scenario catalogue to investigate how other ADS types might have addressed such a traffic conflict. Moreover, lessons learnt deriving from the data collected can be shared among original equipment manufacturers (OEMs) and safety authorities. Ultimately, the ISMR is recognised as a necessary tool to properly tackle the challenges associated with ADS safety assessment given the number of unknowns that might remain undisclosed by leveraging the traditional homologation validation scheme only.

</details>


### [32] [CAP: Continuously Adaptive Perception-Blind Humanoid Locomotion via Learned Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11553)
*Hongjin Chen,Zijun Xu,Shihao Ma,Yi Zhao,Xilai Liu,Ke Ma,Wei Zhang,Chunyang Xie,Pengfei Li,Jieru Zhao,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAP是一种单阶段人形机器人运动策略，通过将感知世界模型编码器训练为学习型去噪器，从受损深度图中重建干净深度，并结合共激活本体感受变分编码器提供无深度依赖的身体状态信息，从而在复杂地形上实现感知鲁棒的运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在复杂地形运动时需要前瞻性外部感知来预判障碍物，但该信号在实际部署中不可靠，会出现部分和间歇性失效。现有感知策略通常假设深度观测是干净且同分布的；而近期统一感知与盲控的尝试多以独立子策略进行路由或切换，未能利用部分损坏深度中仍可恢复的信息。

Method: 提出单阶段人形运动策略CAP：（1）感知世界模型编码器作为学习型去噪器，从受损输入重建干净深度；（2）共激活本体感受变分编码器提供无深度依赖的身体状态信息；（3）耦合训练方案，将世界模型输入端的深度噪声课程与策略侧潜变量的世界模型特征丢弃相结合，使策略暴露于完整的感知质量谱系。

Result: 仿真中，当深度信息仍然有效时，CAP达到或优于感知基线；随着感知质量恶化，其性能下降比二值切换基线更平滑。在Unitree G1上，受控试验及室内外部署验证了在间歇性遮挡、真实传感器损坏和户外深度伪影下的感知鲁棒运动能力。

Conclusion: CAP通过单阶段、去噪与本体感受协同的框架，避免了子策略切换并充分利用部分损坏深度中的可恢复信息，实现了跨感知质量谱系的平滑降级与真实环境下的鲁棒人形运动。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion across complex terrain demands forward-looking exteroception to anticipate obstacles, yet this signal is unreliable in real-world deployment, failing partially and intermittently. Existing perceptive policies often assume that depth observations remain clean and in-distribution, while recent attempts to unify perceptive and blind control typically route or switch between separate sub-policies, leaving recoverable information in partially corrupted depth unexploited. We instead propose CAP, a single-stage humanoid locomotion policy that recovers this signal with a perceptive world-model encoder trained as a learned denoiser to reconstruct clean depth from a corrupted input, together with a co-active proprioceptive variational encoder that supplies depth-free body-state information. A coupled training recipe pairs a depth-noise curriculum on the world-model input with world-model feature dropout on the policy-facing latent, exposing the policy to failures across the entire perception-quality spectrum. In simulation, CAP matches or improves upon perceptive baselines when depth remains informative, and degrades more smoothly than a binary-switching baseline as perception worsens. On the Unitree G1, controlled trials and indoor-outdoor deployments demonstrate perception-robust locomotion under intermittent occlusion, real-sensor corruption, and outdoor depth artifacts.

</details>


### [33] [Memory as Plans: World-Action Modeling with Memory-Grounded Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11561)
*Sizhe Zhao,Haozhe Xie,Weiyu Zhao,Chenchu Zhang,Huan Wang,Chenyang Wang,Qinglin Liu,Shengping Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MaP-WAM框架，将依赖记忆的世界-动作建模分解为记忆锚定的规划与计划条件执行，用长期多模态情景记忆作为规划时证据，实现长时程非马尔可夫操作，在RMBench达83.3%、真机达78.0%，且执行器推理延迟随历史增长近似恒定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流机器人策略采用马尔可夫形式，但许多复杂真实操作任务本质是非马尔可夫的，需要超出当前观测的长时程记忆。现有记忆机制依赖语言摘要、增长的视觉窗口或其组合，可能丢失细粒度视觉证据，或在历史覆盖与执行效率之间权衡。

Method: MaP-WAM将记忆表示为包含语言指令与稀疏视觉上下文的历史片段记录，把情景记忆转换为紧凑计划（下一段级语言计划与对应视觉引导）；由World-Action-Progress（WAP）模型在未知时长内执行计划，推理时联合预测动作块与执行进度，并通过计划-观测对齐校准进度以实现自适应片段切换与闭环上下文更新；执行器上下文长度固定，结构化注意力支持规划与执行中的KV缓存。

Result: 在RMBench上取得83.3%成功率（SOTA），真机任务达78.0%成功率，且随任务历史增长执行器推理延迟近似恒定。

Conclusion: MaP-WAM通过将长期多模态情景记忆转化为规划时证据、并保持执行器上下文固定，兼顾了长时程记忆覆盖与执行效率，为记忆依赖的非马尔可夫操作提供了有效且高效的世界-动作建模方案。

Abstract: Mainstream robotic policies often adopt a Markovian formulation, but many complex real-world manipulation tasks are inherently non-Markovian, requiring long-horizon memory beyond the current observation. Existing memory mechanisms often rely on language summaries, growing visual windows, or their combinations, and may therefore lose fine-grained visual evidence or face a trade-off between history coverage and execution efficiency. We introduce MaP-WAM, a Memory-as-Plans framework that decomposes memory-dependent world-action modeling into memory-grounded planning and plan-conditioned execution, and uses long-term multimodal episodic context as planning-time evidence rather than repeatedly conditioning the executor on the full history. MaP-WAM represents memory as completed segment records containing language instructions and sparse visual context, and converts this episodic memory into compact plans comprising the next segment-level language plan and corresponding visual guidance. A World-Action-Progress (WAP) model executes each plan over an unknown duration by jointly predicting action chunks and corresponding execution progress at inference time, calibrating predicted progress through plan-observation alignment for adaptive segment transitions and closed-loop context updates. MaP-WAM keeps the executor context length fixed, while structured attention further enables key-value caching in both planning and execution. MaP-WAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on RMBench with an 83.3% success rate and attains 78.0% success on real-robot tasks, while maintaining approximately constant executor inference latency as task history grows.

</details>


### [34] [Quasi-static analysis of passive stability in a novel underactuated multi-finger hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11579)
*Léonie Plancoulaine,Sylvain Guégan,Franck Plestan,Damien Chablat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出准静力学解析方法评估欠驱动多指手的被动稳定性，并设计含差动弹簧滑块机构的三指手，分析其对抓取行为及不同尺寸物体在柱形/球形抓取下的稳定平衡构型影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠驱动手可用较少驱动器实现自适应稳健抓取，但被抓物体的稳定平衡位姿预测困难，缺少有效的被动稳定性分析手段。

Method: 采用准静力学解析方法分析被动稳定性；设计一种融合差动弹簧-滑块机构的新型三指手架构；研究差动机构对整体抓取行为的影响，并分析物体尺寸对柱形与球形两类典型抓取的稳定平衡构型的作用。

Result: 给出了差动机构对抓取行为影响的分析，并揭示了物体尺寸对两类典型抓取稳定平衡构型的影响规律（摘要未提供具体数值结果）。

Conclusion: 该准静力学解析框架结合差动弹簧-滑块三指手，可用于评估欠驱动多指手的被动稳定抓取，并预测不同尺寸物体下的稳定平衡位姿。

Abstract: Underactuated robotic hands achieve adaptive and robust grasping with a reduced number of actuators, but predicting the stable equilibrium pose of the grasped object remains a significant challenge. This paper introduces a quasi-static analytical approach to assess passive stability in underactuated multi-finger hands. A novel three-finger hand architecture integrating a differential spring-loaded slider mechanism is introduced, enabling versatile and adaptive grasping. The study focuses on how the differential mechanism influences the overall grasp behavior and analyzes the effect of object size on the stable equilibrium configurations for two canonical grasp types: cylindrical and spherical.

</details>


### [35] [Contact-Aware Incremental Model Predictive Control for an Underactuated Aerial Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11661)
*Darwin Liu,Tamas Keviczky,Sihao Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向欠驱动平台的接触感知空中书写控制框架，结合NMPC与全身INDI，实现了五自由度末端位姿与接触力同步跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中书写需要末端执行器在接触表面对位置、法向力和姿态进行精确控制，同时需应对摩擦与气动扰动。已有方案通常依赖全驱动平台、复杂机械臂或专用力/力矩传感器，成本与复杂度高。本文旨在证明仅用标准欠驱动四旋翼配简单刚性单连杆臂即可完成该任务。

Method: 框架由两部分组成：一是非线性模型预测控制（NMPC），用于在小参考穿透深度下精确跟踪末端执行器位置与法向力，并在不同控制器整定下保持一致性能；二是全身增量非线性动态逆（INDI），用于在接触过程中抵抗摩擦力和气动扰动。验证平台为四旋翼空中机械臂，搭载刚性、单连杆、单自由度机械臂，进行仿真与真实实验，涵盖垂直与倾斜表面、多种参考力、不同摩擦条件及风扰。

Result: 实验表明，在标准欠驱动四旋翼配简单刚性单连杆臂的条件下，能够实现鲁棒的同时五自由度末端位姿与接触力跟踪，且无需全驱动平台、复杂机械臂或专用力/力矩传感。

Conclusion: 该工作证明接触感知空中书写可在低硬件复杂度的欠驱动平台上实现，NMPC与全身INDI的结合是兼顾精度与鲁棒性的有效方案。

Abstract: We present a robust contact-aware control framework for aerial writing on an underactuated platform. The framework combines nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) for accurate end-effector position and normal-force tracking at small reference penetration depths, with consistent performance across controller tunings, with whole-body incremental nonlinear dynamic inversion (INDI) for robustness to frictional and aerodynamic disturbances during contact. The proposed controllers are validated on a quadrotor-based aerial manipulator with a rigid, single-link, one-degree-of-freedom (DoF) arm in simulation and real-world experiments. The aerial writing experiments span vertical and inclined surfaces, multiple reference forces, different friction conditions, and wind disturbances. The results demonstrate that robust simultaneous five-DoF end-effector pose and contact-force tracking is achievable on a standard underactuated quadrotor with a simple, rigid, single-link arm, without requiring a fully actuated platform, a complex arm, or dedicated force/torque sensing.

</details>


### [36] [ActSafeGuard: Differentiable and Training-Aligned Constraint Enforcement for Flow-Matching Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11697)
*Jianming Ma,Rongjun Jin,Xiaxi Si,Yang Zhang,Yiheng Li,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ActSafeGuard，一种可微且训练对齐的安全防护层，将硬动作可行性融入流匹配策略学习，在 π_{0.5} 与 Fast-WAM 上实现 100% 逐步安全率并保持或提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 与 WAM 虽在通用机器人操作上表现优异，但生成动作可能违反硬物理约束，导致不安全或不可部署。现有安全方法要么只优化统计安全目标、缺乏逐步确定性保证，要么仅在推理阶段纠正不安全动作，造成策略训练与执行不一致。

Method: ActSafeGuard 作为面向流匹配策略的可微、训练对齐安全防护层，把硬动作可行性直接纳入策略学习，而非仅作为推理期外部模块；通过解析式射线缩放（ray-scaling）算子设计，实现边界感知梯度，引导模型自然学习受约束流形。

Result: 在多个标准基础骨干（π_{0.5}、Fast-WAM）及多种任务上的大量实验表明，ActSafeGuard 稳定达到 100% 逐步安全率，同时完全保持甚至提升任务成功率。

Conclusion: ActSafeGuard 为安全具身智能部署提供了一种可扩展、侵入性极小的解决方案，兼顾硬约束满足与任务性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and World-Action Models (WAMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in general-purpose robotic manipulation, yet their generated actions may violate hard physical constraints and therefore be unsafe or infeasible for deployment. Existing safety approaches either optimize statistical safety objectives without deterministic per-step guarantees or correct unsafe actions only during inference, creating a mismatch between policy training and execution. We introduce ActSafeGuard, a differentiable and training-aligned safeguard layer for flow-matching based policies. ActSafeGuard integrates hard action feasibility into policy learning, not merely treating safety as an inference-time external component. Through an analytical ray-scaling operator design, ActSafeGuard enables boundary-aware gradients to guide the model to naturally learn constrained manifolds. Extensive experiments on multiple standard foundation backbones ($π_{0.5}$ and Fast-WAM) across various tasks demonstrate that ActSafeGuard consistently achieves a $100\%$ step safety rate while fully preserving or even boosting task success rates, providing a scalable and minimally invasive solution for safe embodied AI deployment.

</details>


### [37] [Aerodynamic Prior-Free Coordinated Trajectory Generation and Tracking Control for a Tail-Sitter UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11698)
*Erchao Rong,Zihao Liu,Junning Liang,Jianguo Wang,Xiao Jie,Haoran Fu,Ziliang Chen,Ximin Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向尾座式无人机的轨迹生成与跟踪一体化控制框架，无需特定机体的气动先验，通过分阶段气动建模策略实现全飞行包络的高精度轨迹跟踪，并完成真实飞行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尾座式无人机在全飞行包络内气动特性高度非线性，且跨越大迎角与悬停/巡航过渡区域，传统方法依赖针对特定机体的气动辨识实验，成本高、泛化性差，难以实现精确的轨迹跟踪控制。

Method: 针对规划与跟踪的不同功能特性，采用分阶段气动建模策略：在协调飞行假设下利用 phi-theory 模型推导解析的微分平坦映射用于轨迹生成；同时为预测控制建立简化但局部精确的模型，以支持实时气动参数估计。整个框架不依赖机体特定的气动先验。

Result: 通过仿真与温和风况下具有挑战性的真实飞行测试进行了广泛评估，在测试的气动条件下展现出高精度跟踪能力与良好的适应性。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个在尾座式无人机完整飞行包络的测试飞行区间内、不依赖气动辨识实验而实现精确轨迹跟踪的真实飞行演示；框架源代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper presents a coordinated trajectory generation and tracking control framework for a tail-sitter unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV), which does not require aerodynamic priors identified for a specific airframe while addressing the challenge of flight control under highly nonlinear aerodynamics across the full flight envelope. The core innovation lies in employing phase-specific aerodynamic modeling strategies for planning and tracking, tailored to their distinct functional characteristics, without requiring airframe-specific aerodynamic priors. Specifically, the phi-theory model under coordinated flight is employed to derive an analytic differential flatness mapping, and a simplified but locally accurate model is established for predictive control to enable real-time aerodynamic parameter estimation. The proposed framework is evaluated extensively through both simulation and challenging real-world flight tests under mild wind conditions, showing high-precision tracking and adaptability across the tested aerodynamic conditions. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first real-world demonstration of accurate trajectory tracking over tested flight regimes spanning the full envelope of a tail-sitter UAV without relying on aerodynamic identification campaigns. The source code of our framework is available at: https://github.com/SYSU-HILAB/AP-PnC.

Abstract: Muscle-driven locomotion provides a physically grounded approach to generating realistic human movement. However, achieving both physiological plausibility and adaptability to changes in musculoskeletal capacity and external disturbances remains a fundamental challenge. To address this limitation, we propose a Reflex-Informed Neuromuscular Reinforcement Learning framework for muscle-driven locomotion. Within this framework, a fixed phase-dependent reflex controller serves as the underlying neuromuscular control mechanism, while the reinforcement learning policy produces four biomechanically meaningful residual parameters to modulate key reflex gains and thresholds associated with hip swing, knee support, and ankle propulsion according to the current state. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework generates physiologically plausible locomotion with improved kinematic accuracy and dynamic consistency, as well as better bilateral symmetry and stride-to-stride consistency under nominal walking conditions. The learned policy remains robust under muscle weakness and external perturbations without retraining.

Method: 提出SEED-UMI框架：人类与机器人佩戴同一外骨骼，关节编码器成为物理共享测量，安装于外骨骼的腕部相机在人类数据采集与机器人策略rollout中观察同一外部机构。由此将重定向转化为成对的跨具身监督，并允许策略直接在原始外骨骼中心腕部图像上训练，无需分割或修复。

Abstract: Imitation learning for dexterous hands is bottlenecked by the difficulty of collecting contact-rich demonstrations that transfer faithfully to the robot. Prior wearable-exoskeleton systems record only on the human side and retarget via open-loop mappings calibrated in free space, which degrade under contact. We present SEED-UMI, a framework in which both the human and the robot wear the same exoskeleton: joint encoders become a physically shared measurement, and wrist cameras mounted to the exoskeleton observe the same outer mechanism during both human data collection and robot policy rollouts. This turns retargeting into paired cross-embodiment supervision and lets policies train directly on raw exoskeleton-centric wrist images, without segmentation or inpainting. On five contact-rich tasks, SEED-UMI achieves 3.0x greater data collection efficiency than exoskeleton-based teleoperation and a 70.0% average rollout success rate.

</details>

Abstract: Advanced crop monitoring inside greenhouses is becoming one of the primary objectives of research centers. High-performance sensors, such as LiDAR or stereo cameras, have traditionally been employed for this purpose, though these often have a high cost. This work proposes a Visual-SLAM system using a monocular camera, which is significantly more cost-effective and specifically tailored for agricultural applications, such as mapping tomato crops in a greenhouse. Tests were carried out on a real tomato bunch, located in the Agroconnect experimental greenhouse. A ROS 2 Humble node was developed to run on the robot in order to capture images of these crops, which were then stored for offline processing. To generate a 3D mapped model for the crop in the greenhouse, the GLOMAP mapper, based on Structure-From-Motion, was integrated with the Hierarchical Localization toolbox. This initial mapping is a foundation for future, more advanced algorithms to analyze growth patterns, and optimize agricultural management. The system leverages a hierarchical localization paradigm based on a coarse-to-fine strategy: it first performs global retrieval to generate location hypotheses, then combines local features within the identified candidate regions. The results show a correct identification of the tomato cluster, correctly characterising the tomato that is occluded and inaccessible by classical vision technologies. The reconstructed 3D model was further validated against manual ground-truth measurements of fruit size, centroid position, and orientation, confirming the geometric accuracy of the proposed low-cost monocular pipeline.

</details>

*Kai Stewart,Yasunori Toshimitsu,Robert K. Katzschmann*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出基于物理机器人上“手与物体组合系统”的实时任务雅可比估计的具身控制方法。控制器仅使用笔记本CPU，在约18秒初始化后开始握笔书写，并持续在线自适应；不依赖解析的手-物运动学/接触模型、仿真训练或预采集任务示范。同一估计器/控制器公式在三个拟人机器人手系统（一个物理、两个仿真）上验证，实现与具体本体无关的表述。

Abstract: Dexterous in-hand manipulation of a grasped object with an anthropomorphic hand is an unsolved frontier for robot dexterity. The contact-richness and highly dynamic nature of object-hand interactions tend to require extensive modeling or data-collection efforts for learning-based approaches. Modern simulators used for reinforcement learning (RL) cannot fully replicate the required contact complexity, while collecting dexterous demonstrations for imitation learning (IL) remains an open problem. In this research, we present an embodied control approach based on real-time task Jacobian estimation of the combined hand and object system on the physical robot. Using only the CPU on a laptop, the proposed controller begins in-hand pen writing after approximately 18 s of initialization and continues to adapt online, without an analytic hand--object kinematic/contact model, simulation training, or precollected task demonstrations. We demonstrate that the same estimator/controller formulation works on three anthropomorphic robotic hand systems (one physical, two simulated) to show human-like, in-hand articulation of a grasped pen by an embodiment-independent formulation. Sub-millimeter in-plane precision (mean 0.6 mm across runs) is achieved across letters and shapes written in the air and on paper on a physical robot. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of an anthropomorphic hand writing arbitrary single-stroke trajectories with a grasped pen through purely in-hand motion, and it showcases an alternative to compute- and data-heavy approaches such as RL and IL for achieving dexterous manipulation through computationally simple and data-efficient algorithms.

</details>


### [42] [Learning Agent-based Model Predictive Control for Holistic Vehicle Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11871)
*Jiaming Zhong,Reza Valiollahi Mehrizi,Mohammad Pirani,Chao Yu,Alireza Kasaiezadeh,Yash Vardhan Pant,Amir Khajepour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LAMPC（learning agent-based MPC）混合控制方案，将模型驱动的AMPC与数据驱动学习结合，用GPR预测未知贡献并借助预测方差构建软机会约束，实现多智能体系统整体性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AMPC依赖所有智能体贡献已知的预测精度，过于理想化，实际中未知贡献难以获取，导致最优性受限。

Method: 结合模型驱动的AMPC与数据驱动学习；以在线数据管理策略增强的GPR为学习核心预测未知贡献；设计多步预测机制沿预测时域发挥GPR潜力；用预测均值补全MPC系统模型；基于预测方差构建软机会约束的随机框架保证安全与可行性。

Result: 仿真与实验表明，LAMPC优于传统AMPC：在已充分学习的场景中跟踪性能更高，在欠学习场景中始终保证约束满足；混合方案实时实现高效，且灵活适配任意控制智能体拓扑。

Conclusion: LAMPC通过模型与学习融合，在保证约束满足的同时提升了多智能体系统整体控制性能，兼具实时性与拓扑灵活性。

Abstract: Agent-based model predictive control (AMPC) has recently been proposed as a distributed scheme that collaborates with all agents to achieve optimal holistic performance. However, its optimality highly depends on the prediction accuracy that requires all agents or their contributions to be known, which is too idealistic for actual implementation. This research proposes a novel practical hybrid control scheme - learning agent-based MPC (LAMPC), combining the model-based AMPC approach and data-based learning methods to improve the holistic vehicle performance for multi-agent systems. The Gaussian process regression (GPR) enhanced by an online data management strategy serves as the learning core to predict unknown contributions. A novel multi-step prediction mechanism leverages the GPR learning potential along the horizon. The predicted mean, representing the learned unknown contributions, completes the system model in the MPC for more accurate control. Meanwhile, a stochastic framework is formulated to guarantee control safety and feasibility using soft chance constraints based on the prediction variance. Both simulations and experiments show that, with the learning capability, LAMPC outperforms the traditional AMPC. LAMPC can achieve higher tracking performance in well-learned scenarios and always guarantee constraint satisfaction even in less-learned scenarios. Moreover, the proposed hybrid control scheme is efficient for real-time implementation and is flexible to any control agent topology.

</details>


### [43] [UniMPA: A Unified Memory-Prediction-Action Model via Action-Grounded Transition Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11875)
*Wei Li,Rui Shao,Jie He,Lingsen Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 UniMPA，一个统一的记忆-预测-动作模型，通过共享的“动作锚定过渡接口”解决 VLA 模型中观测到动作学习的过渡可实现性差距，涵盖过渡歧义、预测-执行不匹配、经验-实现不匹配三大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型虽提升了机器人操作能力，但观测到动作的学习受限于“过渡可实现性差距”：视觉相似观测可能对应不同操作阶段（过渡歧义）；视觉上合理的预测未来未必物理可实现（预测-执行不匹配）；历史可执行动作模式在当前场景未必能实现目标过渡（经验-实现不匹配）。三者紧密耦合。

Method: 提出 UniMPA，通过共享的动作锚定过渡接口统一解决三问题：(i) Persistent-Selective Future Prediction 建模意图未来状态演化——持续潜变量流追踪任务级进度，过渡关键像素流借助记忆锚定预测选择性解析细粒度交互变化；(ii) 预测过渡查询时序 Visual-Action Memory Bank，检索历史已实现的视觉-动作经验，使预测以可执行证据为依据；(iii) Action-Visual Memory Bank 从历史动作演化中检索视觉锚定动作原型，Prototype-Biased Flow 将流源偏向历史支持的动作流形以实现上下文感知细化。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验数据或量化结果。方法层面预期效果为：缓解过渡歧义、以可执行证据约束未来预测、并通过原型偏置实现上下文自适应的动作精化。

Conclusion: UniMPA 以记忆-预测-动作统一框架应对观测到动作学习的过渡可实现性差距，核心在于共享的动作锚定过渡接口与双记忆库机制（Visual-Action Memory Bank、Action-Visual Memory Bank），使预测具备可执行性、动作具备场景适应性。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have improved robotic manipulation, yet observation-to-action learning remains limited by a fundamental transition realizability gap, manifested in three tightly coupled problems: (i) Transition ambiguity. Visually similar current observations may correspond to different manipulation phases and imply different subsequent transitions. (ii) Prediction--execution mismatch. A visually plausible predicted future observation does not necessarily correspond to a physically realizable transition. (iii) Experience--realization mismatch. A historically executable action pattern may not necessarily realize the intended transition in the current scene and therefore requires context-aware adaptation. Accordingly, we propose UniMPA, a Unified Memory-Prediction-Action model that addresses these problems through a shared action-grounded transition interface. (i) UniMPA introduces Persistent-Selective Future Prediction to resolve transition ambiguity by modeling the intended future state evolution. A persistent latent stream continuously tracks task-level progress, while a transition-critical pixel stream selectively resolves fine-grained interaction changes through memory-grounded prediction. (ii) To assess the physical executability of the anticipated transition, the predicted transition queries a temporal Visual-Action Memory Bank. The bank retrieves historically realized visual-action experience, grounding future prediction in executable evidence. (iii) To adapt executable experience to the current scene, an Action-Visual Memory Bank retrieves visually grounded action prototypes from historical action evolution. Prototype-Biased Flow then shifts the flow source toward a historically supported action manifold for context-aware refinement.

</details>


### [44] [EVPeriscope: Extended Perception across Aerial and Ground Vehicles with Event-based Propeller Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11920)
*Dexter Ong,Vijay Kumar,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EVPeriscope：地面机器人用朝上的事件相机检测四旋翼桨叶高频视觉特征，实现检测、定位与200Hz闭环控制，成为地面机器人的“延伸感知”；在15 mph风、昼夜及密集植被遮挡下验证了定位与导航能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地异构团队的紧密协同需要可靠的相对定位，但传统帧式相机与基准标记对运动模糊、光照变化敏感，且受载荷约束，难以胜任。

Method: 在地面机器人上安装朝上的事件相机，检测四旋翼螺旋桨的高频视觉特征，构建事件驱动的感知系统完成检测与相对定位；控制回路完全基于机载感知与计算，以200 Hz运行，形成“袋鼠式”地空系统。

Result: 在风速高达15 mph的野外条件下、白天与夜间均完成实验；在密集植被导致地面机器人传感器被遮挡时，仍支持定位与闭环导航。

Conclusion: 事件相机可通过螺旋桨高频特征实现稳健的空地相对定位，并使四旋翼在地面机器人传感器退化或被遮挡时充当其延伸感知系统。

Abstract: Reliable relative localization between aerial and ground robots is a key requirement for tightly coordinated heterogeneous teams. This can be difficult to do using conventional frame-based cameras and fiducial markers because they are sensitive to motion blur, lighting variations, and payload constraints. This paper presents EVPeriscope, an event-based perception system that enables detection, localization and control of a quadrotor using an upward-facing event camera on a ground robot by detecting the high-frequency visual signature of its propellers. This system allows the quadrotor to function as an extended perception system for the ground robot when onboard sensors exhibit degradation or occlusion. We demonstrate the capabilities of this marsupial ground-aerial system via experiments in challenging field conditions with wind speeds of up to 15 mph, in both daylight and at night. We show that the system supports localization and closed-loop navigation through dense foliage where the ground robot's sensors are occluded. Our control system for the quadrotor operates at 200 Hz entirely with onboard sensing and computation. More details and experiment videos can be found on the project page: https://ongdexter.github.io/evperiscope.

</details>

### [45] [D-modules and Solvable Lie Foliations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10583)
*Ameth Ndiaye*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文从D-模理论视角研究紧流形上可解Lie群$G$-Lie叶状结构，对三类可解Lie叶状结构计算D-不规则性，并揭示和乐群非多循环性与导环高阶上同调非零之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Dathe提出的方法基础上，将叶状结构的几何性质与D-模不变量联系起来。核心动机是寻找和乐群几何（尤其是非多循环性）在D-模不变量中的反映。

Method: 设$V$为紧连通流形、$G$为单连通可解Lie群。对每个奇异叶状结构$\mathcal{I}$，构造$\mathcal{D}_X$-模$\mathcal{M}_{\mathcal{I}}=\mathcal{D}_X/\mathcal{D}_X\cdot\mathcal{I}$及导环$\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{I}}=R\mathcal{H}om_{\mathcal{D}_X}(\mathcal{M}_{\mathcal{I}},\mathcal{M}_{\mathcal{I}})$。计算D-不规则性$\mathrm{D\text{-}irr}(\mathcal{I})$。

Result: 对三类可解Lie叶状结构（正则齐性叶状结构、具非多循环和乐群的Meigniez叶状结构、紧5维流形上的显式叶状结构）计算了D-不规则性；并证明和乐群$\Gamma$的非多循环性体现为$\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{I}}$高阶上同调群的非零。

Conclusion: 建立了和乐群几何与D-模不变量之间的新联系：和乐群的非多循环性可通过导环$\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{I}}$的高阶上同调非零性来刻画。

Abstract: Let $V$ be a compact connected manifold and $G$ a simply connected solvable Lie group.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建面向右删失时间-事件结局的联邦因果元中介框架，针对预定目标人群的自然间接效应，整合各分布源的mediator与outcome机制信息；采用逐站点识别策略刻画数据源异质性，并进行区分outcome相关、mediator相关及交互成分的方差分解；开发联邦一步估计量与联邦目标最大似然估计量，支持数据自适应及机器学习方法估计冗余（nuisance）函数。

Result: 通过数值模拟评估所提估计量的有限样本性能；并应用于法国国家健康数据系统，评估甲氨蝶呤联用在解释TNFi对比IL-12/23抑制剂治疗对银屑病患者治疗持续性的效应中的作用。

Conclusion: 该框架为隐私受限的多中心场景提供了可行的联邦因果中介分析方案，可刻画跨数据源异质性、灵活使用机器学习估计冗余函数，并具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Privacy and data-governance constraints often prevent pooling individual-level data across studies, limiting the use of conventional approaches for causal media- tion analysis in multicenter settings. We propose a federated causal meta-mediation framework for right-censored time-to-event outcomes that enables collaborative es- timation without sharing individual-level data. Our framework targets natural indirect effects in a prespecified population by combining information on mediator and outcome mechanisms across distributed data sources. A site-by-site identifi- cation strategy further allows heterogeneity across data sources to be character- ized, with a variance decomposition separating outcome-related, mediator-related, and interaction components. We develop federated one-step and targeted maxi- mum likelihood estimators that accommodate data-adaptive and machine-learning methods for nuisance-function estimation. The finite-sample performance of the proposed estimators is evaluated through numerical simulations. To illustrate the practical utility of the framework, we apply it on data from the French National Health Data System to evaluate the role of methotrexate coprescription in explain- ing the effect of TNFi versus IL-12/23 inhibitor therapy on treatment persistence among psoriatic patients.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 使用2022年卫星数据与四类地理空间基础模型族生成物理地点的数值表征；以LightGBM预测美国本土82,646个普查区的ACS变量及CDC PLACES的40项慢性病与健康行为结局；在10个留出州上评估性能，并分析残差方差解释及按普查区规模分位数的差异。

Result: 对调查变量预测能力中等（如住房类型R²最高0.54），对残疾、失业、收入差距预测较弱；对健康结局，可解释社会风险指数未解释方差的最高54%，收益最大的是年度体检、关节炎和高血压；40项健康结局的平均总解释方差从最小普查区十分位（0.31）增至最大十分位（0.39）。

Conclusion: 地理空间基础模型能捕捉传统社会风险指数未表征的健康相关地点特征，可有效补充其在流行病学分析中的应用。

Abstract: Area-based social risk indices summarize residents' socioeconomic conditions but incompletely capture physical features of place that may affect health. We evaluated whether numerical representations of physical place produced by four geospatial foundation model families from 2022 satellite data explained residual variance in tract-level associations between the Area Deprivation Index, Social Deprivation Index, and Social Vulnerability Index with health outcomes. We used LightGBM to predict variables from the American Community Survey and 40 chronic disease and health-behavior outcomes from CDC PLACES across 82,646 census tracts in the contiguous United States, evaluating performance across 10 held-out states. Among survey variables, models were moderately predictive of some variables including housing type (R-squared up to 0.54) but weak for disability, unemployment, and income disparity. For health outcomes, models explained up to 54% of variance left unexplained by social risk indices, with the largest gains for annual checkups, arthritis, and high blood pressure. Mean total variance explained by geospatial foundation models across the 40 health-related outcomes increased from 0.31 in the smallest tract-size decile to 0.39 in the largest. Geospatial foundation models capture health-relevant features of place not represented by conventional social risk indices and may usefully augment them in epidemiological analyses.

</details>

### [55] [Almost Linear Universal Point Sets for Planar Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10916)
*Taylor Gordon*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该论文构造了大小为 \(n^{1+o(1)}\) 的通用点集，使任意 \(n\) 顶点平面图都能无交叉直线嵌入其中，将此前二次上界显著改进为近线性规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用点集问题长期存在上界与下界差距：此前已知上界为二次规模，而下界仍为线性，缩小这一差距是平面图绘制理论中的核心问题。

Method: 利用 Bannister、Cheng、Devanny 与 Eppstein 的归约，将通用点集问题转化为 \(213\)-avoiding 置换的 superpattern 构造；再用有序有根森林表示这些置换，并构造一个包含所有此类森林的小型区间族。

Result: 得到大小为 \(n^{1+o(1)}\) 的通用点集，优于先前的二次上界；其证明归结为对该区间族大小的直接界。

Conclusion: 通过置换 superpattern 与有序有根森林区间族的组合方法，显著改进了通用点集的上界；论文中说明 GPT-6 Astra 参与了构造与证明的开发。

Abstract: A point set is universal for planar graphs on $n$ vertices if every such graph has a straight-line drawing without crossings whose vertices belong to the set. We construct universal point sets of size $n^{1+o(1)}$, improving the previous quadratic upper bound. Our construction uses the reduction of Bannister, Cheng, Devanny, and Eppstein from universal point sets to superpatterns for $213$-avoiding permutations. We represent these permutations by ordered rooted forests and construct a small family of intervals containing every such forest. The result follows from a straightforward bound on the size of the family of intervals. GPT-6 Astra assisted in developing the construction and proof.

</details>


### [56] [Some results on Archdeacon's conjecture for rotation systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11599)
*Arahat Chikkatur,Ji Zeng*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文对Archdeacon关于旋转系统中非平面四元子集数量的猜想进行了计算验证与理论推进，证明了渐近下界并在特定旋转系统类中证实该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hill猜想给出了完全图交叉数的下界，Archdeacon将其强化为组合形式：任意n元旋转系统至少含H(n)个非平面四元子集。本文旨在验证该猜想并给出部分理论进展。

Method: 通过计算机搜索验证n≤10的情形并分析极值旋转系统的可实现性；借助计算机辅助证明给出(8/9-o(1))H(n)的下界；同时给出较弱下界(2/3-o(1))H(n)的手工证明；并推广Felsner关于反极对的工作，研究反极可壳旋转系统。

Result: 计算验证了n≤10时Archdeacon猜想成立且极值旋转系统可由简单画法实现；证明任意旋转系统至少含(8/9-o(1))H(n)个非平面四元子集，手工可证(2/3-o(1))H(n)；并证明猜想对反极可壳旋转系统成立。

Conclusion: Archdeacon猜想在小规模及反极可壳类旋转系统中成立，计算机辅助给出接近猜想的渐近下界，为完全图交叉数下界的组合强化提供了新证据与方法。

Abstract: A rotation system on $n$ elements assigns to each element a cyclic order of the other $n-1$ elements. A four-element subset is non-planar if its induced rotation system cannot be realized by a crossing-free drawing of $K_4$. As a combinatorial strengthening of Hill's conjecture on the crossing number of the complete graph, Archdeacon conjectured that every rotation system on $n$ elements has at least $H(n)=\frac{1}{4} \lfloor\frac {n}{2}\rfloor \lfloor\frac{n-1}{2}\rfloor \lfloor\frac{n-2}{2}\rfloor \lfloor\frac{n-3}{2}\rfloor$ non-planar four-element subsets.
  We computationally verify Archdeacon's conjecture for $n\leq 10$ and show that every extremal rotation system in these orders is realizable by a simple drawing. With computer assistance, we prove that every rotation system on $n$ elements has at least $(8/9 - o(1)) H(n)$ non-planar four-element subsets. We also present a proof by hand for a weaker lower bound of $(2/3-o(1)) H(n)$. Finally, extending recent work of Felsner on antipodal pairs in drawings, we show that Archdeacon's conjecture holds for antipodally shellable rotation systems.

</details>

Main category: math.NT

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将多变元多项式GCD归约为多个单变元多项式GCD，在处理高次数、多变量但GCD项数较少的多项式时效率受限，因此需要一个更高效的算法并给出明确的比特复杂度分析。

Method: 采用标准的"多变元归约到单变元"思路，但只归约到一次单变元多项式GCD。核心是一种新的变量替换，将多变元多项式化为"分离"形式，即主变元中的系数均为单项式。随后进行显式比特复杂度分析，并在Maple中实现该算法。

Result: 新算法的比特复杂度是输入与输出规模以及各变量次数界的多项式。Maple实现表明，该算法对于高次数、变量数多、但GCD项数较少的多项式是高效的。

Conclusion: 所提算法在理论上具有多项式级比特复杂度，在实践上对特定结构（高次、多变元、GCD项数少）的多项式表现高效，是一种可行的多变元多项式GCD求解方案。

Abstract: We describe a new greatest common divisor (GCD) algorithm for polynomials with integer coefficients. The bit complexity of the new algorithm is polynomial in the input and output sizes and the individual degree bounds.Our algorithm follows the standard approach by reducing multivariate polynomial GCD to univariate polynomial GCD.
  Our algorithm reduces a multivariate polynomial GCD to a single univariate polynomial GCD. The main idea of our algorithm is a new variable substitution which reduces a multivariate polynomial to a separated one, that is, the coefficients in a main variable are all monomials. The explicit bit complexity is analyzed and we have implemented our algorithm in Maple. It is shown that our algorithm is efficient for polynomials with high degree, large number of variables, but small number of terms in GCD.

</details>


### [58] [Sums of distinct divisors of factorials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10902)
*Scott D. Hughes*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了阶乘 $n!$ 的"不同因子表示数"上界 $h(n!)\le(2\log2+o(1))\,n/\log n$，优于此前 $n/(\log n)^{1/2-\varepsilon}$ 的阶次结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进 Tenenbaum–Yokota 引理 4 与 Yokota 1995 年背包问题笔记中关于 $h(N)$ 的阶乘情形上界。

Method: 将递减贪心构造与 Berend–Harmse 的更精细阶乘因子间隙估计相结合；在 $\sqrt{n!}$ 上下分别计数步数，上区间借助倒数因子处理，从而在间隙指数中保留主项系数，得到显式常数 $2\log2$。

Result: $h(n!)\le(2\log2+o(1))\,n/\log n$。

Conclusion: 所得上界在阶次上显著优于既有 $n/(\log n)^{1/2-\varepsilon}$ 型估计。

Abstract: For practical $N$ let $h(N)$ be the least $k$ such that every integer $1\le m\le N$ is a sum of at most $k$ distinct divisors of $N$. We prove $h(n!)\le(2\log2+o(1))\,n/\log n$. This improves the bounds of order $n/(\log n)^{1/2-\varepsilon}$ established in Tenenbaum-Yokota's Lemma 4 and Yokota's 1995 knapsack note. We combine their decreasing greedy construction with the sharper factorial divisor-gap estimate of Berend-Harmse. Counting the steps separately below and above $\sqrt{n!}$, with the upper range handled through reciprocal divisors, retains the leading coefficient in the gap exponent and yields the explicit constant $2\log2$.

</details>

Result: 证明了当 p≡2 (mod 3) 时，定义在 F_p 上的每条普通扭曲Hessian曲线都位于3-火山的表面；给出针对定义在 F_p（p≡2 mod 3）上扭曲Hessian曲线的改进版Sutherland超奇异性检测算法；并证明对定义在 F_{p^2} 上的扭曲Hessian曲线 H(a,d)，其 j-不变量不是 F_{p^2} 中的立方数当且仅当 H(a,d) 是普通的且位于某3-火山的底层。

Conclusion: 扭曲Hessian曲线的3-同源结构给出了普通曲线在3-火山中位置的精确刻画，从而在 p≡2 (mod 3) 的特化情形下加速了超奇异性检测，并推广了超奇异 j-不变量为立方数的已知结论。

Abstract: For any primes $p \neq \ell$, $\ell$-isogeny graphs of ordinary elliptic curves defined over $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$ have a typical structure called $\ell$-volcanoes, and the structure is the core of Sutherland's supersingularity testing algorithm for elliptic curves. In this paper, by exploiting the properties of $3$-isogenies between twisted Hessian curves, we show that when $p \equiv 2 \pmod{3}$ and $\ell = 3$, every ordinary twisted Hessian curve defined over $\mathbb{F}_p$ lies on the surface of the $3$-volcano. As an application, we give an improved version of Sutherland's supersingularity testing algorithm specialized to twisted Hessian curves defined over $\mathbb{F}_p$ with $p \equiv 2 \pmod{3}$. We also give a generalization of the known fact that any supersingular $j$-invariant is a cube in $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$; we show that for any twisted Hessian curve $H(a,d)$ defined over $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$, its $j$-invariant is not a cube in $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$ if and only if $H(a,d)$ is ordinary and lies on the floor of a $3$-volcano.

</details>


### [60] [Effective estimates for exponential sums with multiplicative coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11070)
*Nicolas Robles*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文在乘性函数满足平均平方界且素数处有界的条件下，改进了 Montgomery–Vaughan 关于乘性函数指数和的经典估计，将第二项中的 $\sqrt{\log R}$ 因子去除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Montgomery–Vaughan 给出了乘性函数指数和的估计，第二项为 $NR^{-1/2}(\log R)^{3/2}$；Bachman 对 $1$-有界函数将其改进为 $NR^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log R\log\log R}$。本文希望在保留 Montgomery–Vaughan 原始系数假设（即仅在素数处有界及平均平方界）的前提下进一步去掉 $\sqrt{\log R}$ 因子。

Method: 证明结合了短区间上的 Brun–Titchmarsh 不等式与由 Carleson–Hunt 定理导出的极大 Fourier 估计；由此得到的局部界允许任意依赖于素数的前缀。此外还给出了更精确的估计，记录与有理数的距离。

Result: 在 $|\alpha-a/q|\le q^{-2}$、$(a,q)=1$、$3\le R\le q\le N/R$ 的条件下，证明 $\sum_{n\le N}f(n)e(n\alpha)\ll_A \frac{N}{\log N}+\frac{N}{\sqrt R}\sqrt{\log\log(3R)}$，隐含常数有效。同时证明了平方根位移依赖关系的最优性。

Conclusion: 作者在保持 Montgomery–Vaughan 系数假设的情况下，成功从 Bachman 的第二项中去除了 $\sqrt{\log R}$ 因子，并证明了该平方根位移依赖的尖锐性。

Abstract: Let $f$ be multiplicative, with $|f(p)|\le A$ at primes and $\sum_{n\le x}|f(n)|^2\le A^2x$ for every $x\ge1$. If $|α-a/q|\le q^{-2}$, $(a,q)=1$, and $3\le R\le q\le N/R$, we prove \[
  \sum_{n\le N}f(n)\operatorname{e}(nα)
  \ll_A \frac{N}{\log N}
  +\frac{N}{\sqrt R}\sqrt{\log\log(3R)} \] with effective implied constants. Montgomery and Vaughan proved this with second term $NR^{-1/2}(\log R)^{3/2}$, and, for $1$-bounded functions, Bachman replaced it by $NR^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log R\log\log R}$. We remove the factor $\sqrt{\log R}$ from Bachman's second term while retaining the original coefficient hypotheses of Montgomery and Vaughan. A more precise estimate records the distance from a rational number. The proof combines the Brun-Titchmarsh inequality on short intervals with maximal Fourier estimates derived from the Carleson-Hunt theorem; the local bounds permit arbitrary prime-dependent prefixes. We also prove sharpness of the square-root displacement dependence.

</details>


### [61] [Rational Approximations for Reciprocals of Multiple Zeta Values and Trivariate Cauchy Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11072)
*Ce Xu,Jianqiang Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过多重对数函数倒数的Laurent展开，研究第一类（Gregory系数）与第二类（Nörlund数）Cauchy数的三变量推广，证明了高阶对数情形下某些Gregory系数为零且序列最终交错，并猜想一般情形下序列最终为正，最后在多重对数与双多重对数情形予以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Gregory系数（第一类Cauchy数）均非零，但作者希望借助多重对数函数倒数的Laurent展开，将Cauchy数推广到更高阶（order）与多变量情形，探讨新序列是否保持经典性质（非零性、最终交错性等），并揭示与zeta值恒等式之间的联系。

Method: 利用多重对数函数倒数的Laurent展开定义三变量Cauchy数推广；对高阶对数情形使用WZ方法证明存在零系数；通过数值证据提出最终正性猜想，并在多重对数与双多重对数特殊情形下加以证明。

Result: 证明了对数情形下：对每个阶ℓ>1，必存在某个ℓ阶Gregory系数为零（与经典情形全非零形成对比）；对固定阶数，序列最终呈交错性；一般情形下新序列猜想最终为正，并有强数值支持，该猜想在多重对数与双多重对数情形被证实。作为副产品，对每个zeta值与双zeta值，找到了一族无穷恒等式，将其倒数表示为有理数与广义积分之和。

Conclusion: 该三变量推广统一并拓展了经典Cauchy数理论：揭示了高阶情形下非零性与交错性的新现象，提出了最终正性猜想并部分证明，同时建立了zeta值与积分表示之间的新恒等式族。

Abstract: In this paper, we will study a trivariate extension of the Cauchy numbers of both the first kind (also called Gregory coefficients) and the second kind (also called Nörlund numbers) via the Laurent expansion of the reciprocal of any positive integer power (which is called the order) of multiple polylogarithms. In the case of logarithm, we will show by the WZ method that for each order $\ell>1$ some Gregory coefficient of order $\ell$ must vanish, in contrast to the fact that all classical Gregory coefficients are nonzero. We also prove in this higher order logarithm case that the sequence is eventually alternating for each fixed order, a property enjoyed by the classical Gregory coefficients. In the most general setting, we conjecture that these new sequences are all eventually positive, which is supported by strong numerical evidence. Finally, we confirm this conjecture in the special case of polylogarithms and double polylogarithms. As a by product, for each zeta value and double zeta value, we find an infinite family of identities expressing its reciprocal as a sum of a rational number and an improper integral.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作建立了经典p进单值定理的相对版本，将局部域的半稳定理论推广到曲线情形，圆盘与圆环的局部结果构成关键中间步骤。

Abstract: We prove that every de Rham $p$-adic local system on a smooth projective curve over a $p$-adic field is potentially semistable; that is, it becomes semistable after pulling back along a finite cover of the curve. Along the way, we also establish a $p$-adic monodromy theorem for de Rham $p$-adic local systems on disks and annuli.

</details>


### [63] [Moments and Non-Vanishing of Maass Form Symmetric Square L-Functions in Short Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11119)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Li此前在短区间(T-H, T+H)（H ≥ T^{18/19+ε}）上得到了三次矩的均值Lindelöf估计；本文旨在放宽对区间长度的限制并改进短区间内中心L-值非零比例的下界。

Abstract: Recently, Li obtained a mean Lindelöf estimate for the cubic moment of the central values of Maass form symmetric square $L$-functions over short intervals $(T-H, T+H)$ of length $H \ge T^{18/19+ε}$. We improve this result by showing that the estimate holds for $H \gg T^{6/7+ε}$. The key ingredient in our proof is a new asymptotic formula for the second twisted moment of Maass symmetric square $L$-functions. Based on this formula, we also improve the lower bound for the proportion of non-vanishing central $L$-values in short intervals. Previously, even under the assumption of the Lindelöf hypothesis for Dirichlet $L$-functions, the proportion of non-vanishing values in intervals of length $H = T^β$ was only known to be at least $\frac{3β-1}{4}$. Abstract: We introduce two independent numerator exponents into the Zeilberger--Zudilin integral and specialize them to \[
  A_1=A_2=\frac{1857}{2785}. \] The resulting integer linear forms in $1$ and $π$ prove \[
  μ(π)<7.101862832357. \] This lowers the Zeilberger--Zudilin upper bound $7.103205334137\ldots$ by more than $0.001342501780$; the difference between the unrounded bounds is $0.0013425017806509\ldots$, approximately $0.01890\%$. The same parameter point is a strict two-dimensional local minimizer of the explicit auxiliary upper-bound function in its admissible arithmetic chamber. This is a local statement about that function, not a claim that the point is a global optimizer among all constructions.

</details>


### [66] [On the bounded-conductor finiteness conjecture in equal characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11456)
*Yufan Luo,Yiqi Xu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究正特征全局函数域上模 $p$ Galois 表示的 Moon–Taguchi 有界导子有限性猜想。作者在附加“存在处处非分歧特征零提升”的条件下证明了有限性定理，并构造反例说明去掉该提升假设后猜想一般不成立，从而在二维情形完整解决了 Moon–Taguchi 问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Moon–Taguchi 有界导子有限性猜想断言：固定维数下，导子有界的模 $p$ Galois 表示的同构类只有有限多个。本文关注等特征（即函数域特征为 $p$）情形，旨在确定该猜想在全局函数域上是否成立，并厘清提升假设的必要性，从而在二维情形给出完整答案。

Method: 在特征为 $p$ 的全局函数域 $K$ 上，考虑连续、半单、处处非分歧且几何的表示 $\rho: G_K \to \mathrm{GL}_n(\overline{\mathbf{F}}_p)$，并附加存在处处非分歧特征零提升的条件，证明同构类有限。为构造反例，对每个 $r \ge 4$ 构造满足条件的全局函数域 $K$，使其具有连续、满射、绝对不可约、处处非分歧且几何的表示 $\rho_r: G_K \twoheadrightarrow \mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbf{F}_{p^r})$，由此得到无穷多个同构类。

Result: (1) 条件性有限性定理成立：存在处处非分歧特征零提升时，满足条件的表示同构类有限。(2) 去掉提升假设后猜想失效：构造出无穷多个全局函数域 $K$，每个都带有对每个 $r \ge 4$ 的满射 $\rho_r: G_K \twoheadrightarrow \mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbf{F}_{p^r})$。(3) 推论：存在处处非分歧、在 $\mathbf{F}_p$ 上正则的 Galois 扩张 $L/K$，其 Galois 群为 $\mathrm{Gal}(L/K) \cong \prod_{r \ge 4} \mathrm{PSL}_2(\mathbf{F}_{p^r})$。

Conclusion: 结合已知的跨特征有限性定理，本文在二维情形完整解决了 Moon–Taguchi 问题：此类扩张在特征为 $p$ 的全局函数域上存在，而在特征异于 $p$ 的全局函数域上不存在。这表明等特征情形与跨特征情形具有本质差异，提升假设是有限性的关键条件。

Abstract: We investigate the equal-characteristic case of the Moon--Taguchi bounded-conductor finiteness conjecture for mod $p$ Galois representations over global function fields. We first establish a conditional finiteness theorem: for any global function field $K$ of characteristic $p$ and any integer $n \ge 1$, there are only finitely many isomorphism classes of continuous, semisimple, everywhere unramified, and geometric representations $ρ: G_K \to \mathrm{GL}_n(\overline{\mathbf{F}}_p)$ that admit an everywhere unramified characteristic-zero lift. Furthermore, we prove that the conjecture fails in general without this lifting hypothesis. Concretely, we construct infinitely many global function fields $K$ of characteristic $p$ admitting a continuous, surjective, absolutely irreducible, everywhere unramified, and geometric representation $ρ_r: G_K \twoheadrightarrow \mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbf{F}_{p^r})$ for each integer $r \ge 4$. As a corollary, we construct an everywhere unramified Galois extension \(L/K\), regular over \(\mathbf F_p\), with Galois group
  \[
  \mathrm{Gal}(L/K)\cong
  \prod_{r\geq4}\mathrm{PSL}_2(\mathbf F_{p^r}).
  \]
  Combined with known cross-characteristic finiteness theorems, this completely resolves the question posed by Moon and Taguchi in dimension two: such extensions exist over global function fields of characteristic \(p\), whereas they cannot exist over global function fields of characteristic different from \(p\).

</details>

Method: 在临界线上研究不可约 GL(1) 与 GL(2) L-函数乘积的混合矩，证明尖锐的矩上界；下界方面提出一种新方法，将 Heath-Brown 的经典方法与 Harper、Heap、Radziwill、Soundararajan 的现代解析工具相结合。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了混合矩理论，给出了尖锐估计并解决了负判别式三次 Dedekind zeta 函数一阶矩阶的未决问题，同时提供了一套可推广的混合矩下界计算新方法。

Abstract: We investigate the mixed moments of products of irreducible $\mathrm{GL}(1)$ and $\mathrm{GL}(2)$ $L$-functions on the critical line, and establish sharp moment bounds for these quantities. In particular this establishes the order of these mixed moments in certain ranges. This determines unconditionally, for the first time, the order of magnitude of the first moment of cubic Dedekind zeta functions with negative discriminant. The proof of the lower bound introduces a new method for computing mixed moments, that is a mix of the classical method of Heath--Brown with the modern machinery of Harper, Heap, Radziwill, and Soundararajan.

</details>


### [68] [Internal congruences modulo powers of $2$ for overpartition tuples with odd parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11806)
*Manjil P. Saikia,Prabal Talukdar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了奇数部分overpartition m-元组的计数函数 $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(n)$ 的 2-adic 性质，证明了模 $2^{i+2}$ 的同余式，并刻画了 2-adic 赋值的等号点恰好为奇平方数，证明方法初等且对 $m$ 一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 overpartition 及其多元组在奇数部分限制下的计数函数的同余性质与 2-adic 行为，是分拆同余理论中的重要课题。已有工作多针对特定 m 或特定模数，缺乏对所有奇 m 统一处理的结果，作者希望给出一个对 m 一致的、初等的框架来刻画 $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^i n)$ 与 $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^{i-1}n)$ 之间的同余关系。

Method: 证明是初等且对 m 一致的。核心是引入一族由三项递推给出的整数多项式，该多项式族统一支配所有涉及的 U-算子恒等式；同时利用一个整除性陈述，使得每迭代一次就提取出一个 2 的幂次，从而逐层提升模 $2$ 的幂次。

Result: 对每个奇数 $m\ge1$ 和每个 $i\ge3$，证明了生成函数同余式 $\sum_{n\ge0}(\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^i n)-\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^{i-1}n))q^n \equiv 2^{i+1}\sum_{k\ge0}q^{(2k+1)^2} \pmod{2^{i+2}}$。由此得到 $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^i n)\equiv \overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^{i-1}n)\pmod{2^{i+1}}$，并且 2-adic 赋值的等号恰好出现在奇平方数处。

Conclusion: 该结果对奇数 m 给出了统一的同余式，将 $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m$ 沿 2 的幂次伸缩的 2-adic 行为归结为奇平方数的贡献，并通过三项递推多项式族与整除性论证提供了初等、统一的证明范式。

Abstract: Let $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(n)$ denote the number of overpartition $m$-tuples of $n$ into odd parts. We prove that for every odd $m\ge1$ and every $i\ge3$, \[\sum_{n\ge0}\Bigl(\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m\bigl(2^in\bigr)-\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m\bigl(2^{i-1}n\bigr)\Bigr)q^n \equiv 2^{\,i+1}\sum_{k\ge0}q^{(2k+1)^2} \pmod{2^{\,i+2}} .\] Thus $\overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^in)\equiv \overline{\mathrm{OPT}}_m(2^{i-1}n)\pmod{2^{i+1}}$, with equality of $2$-adic valuations exactly at the odd squares. The proof is elementary and uniform in $m$: a single family of integer polynomials, given by a three-term recurrence, governs every $U$-operator identity involved, and a divisibility statement supplies one power of $2$ per iteration.

</details>


### [69] [Generalized Frobenius Partitions Modulo Powers of $2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11813)
*Manjil P. Saikia*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了k-着色广义Frobenius剖分数cφ_k(n)的一个模2^m同余关系，并应用于k=18和k=10的情形，确定相关模16和模8的完全刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究k-着色广义Frobenius剖分数的同余性质，推广Chan-Wang-Yang的同余结果，并解决Das-Nath-Sarma（2026）提出的猜想。

Method: 通过建立cφ_k(n)的生成函数模2^m同余公式，利用经典theta函数φ(q)，将cφ_k与cφ_{k/2}的生成函数联系起来。

Result: 证明了：对任意m≥2及k≡2 (mod 2^m)，有∑cφ_k(n)q^n ≡ φ(q)(q^2;q^2)_∞/(q;q)_∞^2 · ∑cφ_{k/2}(n)q^{2n} (mod 2^m)。当k=18时完全确定了cφ_{18}(2n+1)模16，并证明∑cφ_{18}(6n+1)q^n ≡ 4∑q^{r(3r-1)/2} (mod 16)，从而证实了cφ_{18}(30n+19)≡cφ_{18}(30n+25)≡0 (mod 16)的猜想；当k=10时确定了cφ_{10}(2n+1)模8。

Conclusion: 该同余关系统一并推广了已有结果，为k-着色广义Frobenius剖分数的模算术性质提供了有效工具，并解决了近期猜想。

Abstract: Let $cφ_k(n)$ denote the number of $k$-colored generalized Frobenius partitions of $n$. We prove that, for every $m\geq2$ and every $k\equiv2\pmod{2^m}$, \[
  \sum_{n\geq0}cφ_k(n)q^n\equiv\frac{\varphi(q)\,(q^2;q^2)_\infty}{(q;q)_\infty^2}\sum_{n\geq0}cφ_{k/2}(n)q^{2n}\pmod{2^m}, \] where $\varphi(q)$ is the classical theta function. For $m=2$ this recovers a congruence of Chan, Wang, and Yang. Applied with $k=18$, we determine $cφ_{18}(2n+1)$ modulo $16$ completely. In particular, we also prove \[
  \sum_{n\geq0}cφ_{18}(6n+1)q^n
  \equiv4\sum_{r\in\mathbb{Z}}q^{r(3r-1)/2}\pmod{16}, \] which proves the congruences $cφ_{18}(30n+19)\equiv cφ_{18}(30n+25)\equiv0\pmod{16}$ recently conjectured by Das, Nath, and Sarma (2026). It also yields further congruences modulo $16$ and a simple modulo-$8$ characterization that recovers and extends a recent congruence of those authors. As a second application we set $k=10$ and determine $cφ_{10}(2n+1)$ modulo $8$.

</details>


### [70] [Bounded asymptotic bases for linear forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11848)
*Christian Táfula*

Result: 证明：(1) 对任意二元向量 $\mathbf b\neq(1,1)$，均存在这样的集合 $A$；(2) 对若干一般高维族，特别是 $\mathbf b=(u_1,p^du_2,\ldots,p^{(h-1)d}u_h)$ 且 $p\nmid u_1\cdots u_h$，同样存在满足条件的 $A$。即充分大整数均可有界正次地表示为该线性型。

Conclusion: 对系数不含互素限制之外的广泛向量族，存在具有"有界正表示数"性质的 $A$；二元情形完全解决（除 $(1,1)$ 外），高维在 $p$-进指数型族上成立。结果推广了经典受限表示理论，并为一般系数情形的完整刻画提供新工具。

Abstract: For a vector of positive integers $\mathbf{b} = (b_1,\ldots,b_h)$ with $\gcd(b_1,\ldots,b_h) = 1$, we study sets $A \subseteq \mathbb{N}$ for which every sufficiently large integer has a bounded positive number of representations \[ n = b_1 x_1 + \cdots + b_h x_h \qquad (x_1,\ldots,x_h\in A). \] We prove that such a set exists for every binary vector $\mathbf{b} \neq (1,1)$, and for some general higher-dimensional families, including $\mathbf{b} = (u_1, p^d u_2, \ldots, p^{(h-1)d} u_h)$ where $p\nmid u_1\cdots u_h$.

</details>

### [72] [The asymptotic in Waring's problem over function fields beyond twice the degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11888)
*Matthew Hase-Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明函数域 $\mathbb F_q[T]$ 上 Waring 问题的期望渐近式，并在 $n>2d$、特征大于 $(d-1)^2$ 且 $q$ 满足显式下界时给出幂节省误差。核心工具为按奇点轨道余维数分类的聚合次弧估计，结合相交理论控制参数空间次数，使得当 $n\ge(2+\varepsilon)d$ 时对 $q$ 的要求是关于 $d$ 的多项式级下界，次数为 $2+4/\varepsilon$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数域上的 Waring 问题旨在将多项式表示为若干 $d$ 次幂之和，并期望其表示数具有与整数情形类似的渐近主项。先前结果要么适用范围受限，要么对 $q$ 的要求过强（如指数级），无法在大 $d$ 下刻画均匀且最优的渐近范围。本文动机是在 $\mathbb F_q[T]$ 上尽可能最优的范围内证明该渐近式并给出幂节省误差，同时把对有限域大小 $q$ 的依赖降至多项式级。

Method: 采用 Hardy-Littlewood 圆法。主要创新是聚合次弧估计：不再逐个处理次弧，而是按照相关奇异轨迹的余维数对相应泛函进行分类，并借助相交理论（intersection theory）估计这些泛函所参数化空间的次数，从而在次弧上获得聚合型的有效上界。结合该估计与主弧分析，得到期望渐近及幂节省误差。此方法在 $n>2d$ 时生效，特征需大于 $(d-1)^2$，并给出 $q$ 的显式下界。

Result: 在 $n>2d$、特征 $>(d-1)^2$ 以及 $q$ 满足显式下界的条件下，证明了 $\mathbb F_q[T]$ 上 Waring 问题的期望渐近式，并带幂节省误差；该范围对目标多项式一致而言一般是尖锐的。进一步，当 $n\ge(2+\varepsilon)d$ 时，所需的 $q$ 下界仅为 $d$ 的多项式，次数为 $2+4/\varepsilon$。

Conclusion: 本文扩大了函数域 Waring 问题中期望渐近式成立的参数范围，并通过聚合次弧估计显著削弱了对有限域大小 $q$ 的要求。其按奇异轨迹余维数分类并用相交理论控制参数空间次数的思路，为函数域圆法中的次弧估计提供了新的技术框架，也可能适用于其他相关丢番图方程问题。

Abstract: We prove the expected asymptotic in Waring's problem over $\mathbb F_q[T]$, with a power-saving error, whenever $n>2d$, the characteristic is greater than $(d-1)^2$, and $q$ satisfies an explicit lower bound. This range is sharp in general for the expected asymptotic uniformly in the target polynomial.
  Our main new input is an aggregate minor arc estimate: we count functionals according to the codimension of their associated singular loci and use intersection theory to bound the degrees of the resulting parameter spaces. In particular, if $n\ge (2+\varepsilon)d$, the required lower bound on $q$ is polynomial in $d$ of degree $2+4/\varepsilon$.

</details>

TL;DR: 通过三维广义相对论磁流体力学模拟（最长约30秒），发现由快速旋转大质量恒星坍缩形成、并被强磁通量穿过的黑洞可获得10²–10³ km/s的强诞生反冲，其机制来自不对称电磁外流、磁通量爆发及非球对称喷出物的引力拖拽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞诞生反冲通常被认为因质量吸积更多而弱于中子星反冲，且孤立黑洞电磁辐射微弱、观测难以约束，因此其强度与物理来源长期不明确。

Method: 采用时长可达约30秒的三维广义相对论磁流体力学（GRMHD）模拟，研究快速旋转大质量恒星（坍缩星）在强尺度磁场下的坍缩成黑洞过程，并分解不同物理机制对反冲的贡献。

Result: 黑洞可获得10²–10³ km/s量级的诞生反冲；反冲由不对称电磁外流、磁通量爆发和非球对称喷出物的引力拖拽共同塑造，其相对贡献取决于前身星结构、磁通量历史和黑洞自旋；自旋更快的黑洞获得更强且更接近自旋对齐的反冲，主要源于不对称喷流驱动喷出物的引力拖拽；延迟进入磁阻状态会产生更不对称的外流和更强反冲。

Conclusion: 强尺度磁通量既是相对论伽马射线暴喷流及其高能喷流驱动超新星的关键要素，因此诞生反冲可能是磁化坍缩星的自然结果，并可显著影响稠密恒星环境中黑洞的滞留、双星存活、自旋—轨道失准，以及磁化坍缩星在成对不稳定质量间隙内产生高自旋黑洞的可行性。

Abstract: Asymmetric stellar explosions impart an impulse, or natal kick, to their compact remnants by linear momentum conservation. Black hole (BH) natal kicks are often assumed to be weaker than neutron star kicks because of greater mass accretion, and they are harder to constrain observationally because isolated BHs are electromagnetically faint. Using 3D general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulations lasting up to ~30 s, we show that BHs formed from the collapse of rapidly rotating massive stars (collapsars) threaded by strong large-scale magnetic fields can acquire large natal kicks 10^2--10^3 km/s. Asymmetric electromagnetic outflows, magnetic-flux eruptions, and the gravitational pull of aspherical ejecta shape the kick magnitude, and their relative contributions depend on the progenitor structure, magnetic-flux history, and BH spin. More rapidly spinning BHs receive stronger, more nearly spin-aligned kicks, primarily through the gravitational pull of asymmetric jet-driven ejecta. Delayed transitions to the magnetically arrested state produce more asymmetric outflows and can generate even stronger recoils. Because the large-scale magnetic flux required in our models is also a key ingredient of relativistic gamma-ray burst jets and their associated energetic, jet-driven supernovae, natal kicks may be a natural consequence of magnetized collapsars. Such kicks could substantially alter BH retention in dense stellar environments, binary survival, spin-orbit misalignment, and the viability of magnetized collapsars in producing highly spinning BHs within the pair-instability mass gap.

</details>

Abstract: We report the detection of hard X-ray pulsations from the M31 accreting non-ULX X-ray pulsar 3X J0042 (3XMM J004232.1+411314) using archival NuSTAR observations. The source has a spin period of ~3 s and an orbital period of ~4.15 hr, with coherent pulsations previously detected only in XMM-Newton observations. We reanalyse all five publicly available NuSTAR observations by correcting the photon arrival times for binary motion and optimizing the orbital epoch and projected semi-major axis. Coherent pulsations are detected in all five observations. The broad-band pulse profiles show similar morphology across the five observations spanning ~2.3 yr, with no significant long-term evolution. The pulse profile also shows energy dependence, with structured, non-sinusoidal profiles below ~20 keV and a less structured profile at higher energies. The pulse periods show an overall decrease over the ~2.3 yr NuSTAR baseline, continuing the long-term spin-up trend observed in the ~16 yr of XMM-Newton observations, but with a faster average spin-up rate of ~-1.1e-10 s/s compared to the earlier reported ~-6e-11 s/s. The short orbital period and inferred low-mass companion star shows it to be a low-mass X-ray binary containing a magnetized neutron star, with systems such as Her X-1, 3A 1822-371, and XMM J174457-2850.3 providing closer Galactic comparisons. This work represents the first detection of hard X-ray pulsations from a non-ULX extragalactic accreting pulsar.

</details>


### [75] [Teglon: A Pixel-Level Pipeline for Galaxy-Informed Gravitational-Wave Follow-up Planning and Efficiency Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10736)
*D. A. Coulter,P. Darc,C. D. Kilpatrick,R. J. Foley,P. McGill,S. D. Wyatt,R. Hausen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了开源数据库与分析流水线Teglon，用于优化引力波（GW）源的电磁（EM）对应体搜索，通过结合星系完备性度量与GW三维定位体积生成动态观测计划，提升观测效率。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: Teglon基于输入星系目录构建三维星系完备性度量，并与GW事件的三维定位体积进行卷积，生成更新的后验图；可导入任意仪器覆盖范围或从Treasure Map下载，生成动态观测计划，在定向星系指向与区域拼接之间无缝插值；集成Redback计算像素级模型探测效率。

Result: 显著减小搜索电磁对应体的预测搜索面积，提高观测效率；支持自定义、事件特定的观测策略，或对任意EM瞬变模型网格进行回顾性探测效率计算；支持定制科学案例（如优先搜索双黑洞并合的AGN对应体）。

Conclusion: Teglon已支持Gravity Collective及相关项目从LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA第三次观测运行（O3）至O4，并持续开发以支持O5及以后；作者记录了公共仓库谱系与打包，并将Version 2.0作为开源工具提供给社区。

Abstract: We describe Teglon, an open-source database and analysis pipeline engineered to optimize the search for electromagnetic (EM) counterparts to gravitational wave (GW) sources. Teglon constructs a 3D galaxy completeness metric from an input galaxy catalog and convolves it with the 3D localization volume of a GW event, producing an updated posterior map informed by known galaxy distributions. Using Teglon, users can ingest arbitrary instrument footprints --- or download them directly from the Treasure Map --- to generate dynamic observation plans that seamlessly interpolate between targeted galaxy pointing and region tiling. By focusing efforts on high-probability volumes, Teglon significantly reduces the predicted search area to find EM counterparts and increases observational efficiency, especially for small field-of-view (<= 1 deg^2) instruments like the Nancy Grace Roman Space Telescope. Furthermore, Teglon integrates with Redback to calculate pixel-level model detection efficiencies, enabling custom, event-specific observing strategies or retrospective detection efficiencies on an arbitrary grid of EM transient models. Teglon also supports custom science cases, such as prioritizing Active Galactic Nuclei searches for EM counterparts to binary black hole mergers. Having supported Gravity Collective and related programs from the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA third observing run (O3) through O4, Teglon is being continuously developed to support O5 and beyond. We document the public repository lineage and packaging, and provide Version 2.0 as an open-source tool for the community.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 对CRPropa进行定制扩展，在现实的质量、磁荷和初始相空间分布假设下，模拟遗迹磁单极子在银河系环境中的传播，并将其模拟到达方向与Pierre Auger天文台数据对比，采用剖面似然分析结合方向与能量信息。

Conclusion: 低质量磁单极子虽可产生集中于天空小区域的各向异性到达方向，但当前观测数据不支持其显著贡献，所设通量上限对未来搜寻设计与解释具有参考价值。

Abstract: Origin and composition of ultra-high energy cosmic rays are still uncertain, particularly the rare events exceeding the Greisen-Zatsepin-Kuzmin cutoff and whose apparent arrival directions point to the Local Void. This work is an in-depth investigation of the possibility that such cosmic rays contain low-mass magnetic monopoles predicted in several recent theoretical models. Using a custom extension to CRPropa, a state-of-the-art code for modeling cosmic ray propagation, monopoles are tracked in the galactic environment under realistic assumptions on mass, magnetic charge, and initial phase-space distributions. Our simulations demonstrate that relic MMs follow filamentary ``Galactic magnetic funnels'', producing anisotropic arrival directions at Earth. The expected arrival directions are concentrated in a small region of the sky, which has significant implications for the design and interpretation of future searches. A comparison of the simulated arrival directions with the Pierre Auger Observatory dataset shows that the null hypothesis - no monopole contribution - is statistically preferred for the majority of cases. Using a profile-likelihood analysis that incorporates both directional and energy information, we set 90% C.L. upper limits on the integral MM flux of $< 2.1\times10^{-23}$ $\mathrm{cm^{-2}s^{-1}sr^{-1}}$.

</details>


### [77] [XRISM observations of the Perseus cluster along two arms: Chaotic ICM motions probed by resonant scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10845)
*Annie Heinrich,Irina Zhuravleva,Congyao Zhang,Anna Ogorzalek,Ayşegül Tümer,François Mernier,Phillip C. Stancil,Elena Bellomi,Lior Shefler,John ZuHone,Yutaka Fujita,Julie Hlavacek-Larrondo,Yuto Ichinohe,Kyoko Matsushita,Nhut Truong,Shutaro Ueda*
