### [142] [Henstock--Kurzweil Gauge Integral in the Non--Gaussian Regime: A Machine--Verified Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10793)
*Yuri N. Berdinsky*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文用Henstock–Kurzweil规范积分与Chernoff乘积逼近，在Lean 4中机器验证地构造了非高斯泛函积分，证明单模积分解析性质良好、M模影响泛函因子化且有界，并给出四类应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非高斯泛函积分通常依赖Wick旋转、Wiener测度、zeta正则化或从虚时间解析延拓等方法，这些工具在数学严格性与机器验证层面存在困难。作者希望给出一种完全直接、可机器检验的构造。

Method: 采用Henstock–Kurzweil规范积分与Chernoff乘积逼近；研究作用量S(phi)=(1/2)phi^T A phi + lambda*sum_i phi_i^4（A正定）的有限玻色模族；用Chernoff/Lie–Trotter分裂处理自由生成元与非高斯生成元的非交换性；全部结论在Lean 4与Mathlib中形式化。

Result: 证明单模积分I(omega,j,lambda)在[0,∞)上关于耦合lambda有限、严格正、单调且无穷可微，其导数由phi^{4k}的收敛积分给出而非发散微扰级数；M模影响泛函因子化为单模积分之积，并被其高斯值界住；HkNonGaussian.lean不含sorry，仅用propext、Classical.choice、Quot.sound标准公理。

Conclusion: 构造是直接的，不依赖Wick旋转、Wiener测度、zeta正则化或从虚时间解析延拓；并给出Duffing振子、金融局部波动率CEV、Wilson–Cowan神经场、非高斯量子库四类显式公式应用。

Abstract: We develop a machine-checked construction of non-Gaussian functional integrals using the Henstock--Kurzweil gauge integral and Chernoff product approximations. The central object is a finite family of bosonic modes with action S(phi) = (1/2) phi^T A phi + lambda * sum_i phi_i^4, where A is positive definite. We prove that the one-mode integral I(omega, j, lambda) is finite, strictly positive, monotone and infinitely differentiable in the coupling lambda on [0, infinity). Its derivatives are given by convergent integrals of phi^{4k} with the same weight, not by the divergent perturbative series. The M-mode influence functional factorises into one-mode integrals and is bounded by its Gaussian value. A Chernoff / Lie--Trotter splitting handles the non-commutativity of the free and non-Gaussian generators. All statements are formalised in Lean 4 with Mathlib; the accompanying file HkNonGaussian.lean is free of sorry and uses only the standard axioms propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Four illustrative applications are worked out at the level of explicit formulas: the Duffing oscillator, local volatility (CEV) in finance, Wilson--Cowan neural fields, and non-Gaussian quantum reservoirs. The construction is completely direct and does not use Wick rotation, Wiener measure, zeta-regularisation or analytic continuation back from imaginary time.

</details>


### [143] [A Time-Frequency Framework for GKP Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10802)
*Franz Luef,Eduard Ortega*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为格点GKP码建立时间—频率框架：理想码字实现于调制空间 M^∞，经向量值Zak变换与连续syndrome环面上的有限逻辑纤维等同；多窗Gabor分析将逻辑向量表示为对偶格系数块，证明归一化块映射为等距、给出显式恢复投影与稳定逻辑重构，并构造可归一化GKP逼近算子，建立弱-*收敛与渐近等距编码，最后从系数块平移相位关系恢复位移syndrome并量化其稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP码的理想码字在物理上不可归一化，且其连续syndrome结构与有限维逻辑信息之间的对应、噪声下逻辑重构的稳定性尚缺乏严格的数学框架。作者希望用时间—频率分析与Gabor框架为格点GKP码提供统一的严格表述。

Method: 采用时间—频率分析：在调制空间 M^∞ 中实现理想码字，用向量值Zak变换将其与连续syndrome环面上的有限逻辑纤维等同；再用多窗Gabor分析把逻辑向量表示为对偶格上的有限系数块，研究其块映射的等距性与恢复投影；构造格包络Gabor乘子型可归一化GKP逼近算子；并由系数块间平移的相位关系提取位移syndrome。

Result: 证明归一化块映射是等距映射，得到显式的恢复投影，并导出稳定的逻辑重构；构造出可归一化的GKP逼近算子，证明其弱-*收敛及渐近等距编码性质；成功从平移系数块的相位关系中恢复位移syndrome，并给出其在加性扰动下的稳定性定量刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作以调制空间、Zak变换与多窗Gabor框架为工具，为格点GKP码提供了自洽的时间—频率理论：逻辑信息可由有限系数块稳定编码与恢复，不可归一化的理想码字可通过Gabor乘子逼近，位移syndrome可由相位关系稳健读出。

Abstract: We develop a time--frequency framework for lattice GKP codes in which ideal codewords are realized in the modulation space $M^\infty$ and identified, through a vector-valued Zak transform, with a finite logical fibre over the continuous syndrome torus. Multi-window Gabor analysis then represents the logical vector by a finite block of adjoint-lattice coefficients. We prove that the normalized block map is an isometry, obtain an explicit recovering projection, and derive stable logical reconstruction. We further construct normalizable GKP approximants as lattice-envelope Gabor multipliers and establish weak-$*$ convergence and asymptotically isometric encoding. Finally, we recover displacement syndromes from phase relations between translated coefficient blocks and quantify their stability under additive perturbations.

</details>


### [144] [Probabilistic representation and limit theorems for particle numbers of quasi-free states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10840)
*Fanch Coudreuse,Simone Rademacher,Oliver Tse*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了局部相互作用玻色准自由态的粒子数可分解为无穷多个独立几何分布随机变量之和，并据此导出了指数尾部界、小数定律、大偏差原理与中心极限定理，应用于玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚中量子耗尽统计的精确刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部相互作用玻色准自由态广泛出现于数学物理多个领域，其粒子数分布的统计性质此前仅有物理学文献中的预测，缺乏严格的数学刻画。作者希望建立严格的概率表示，并由此系统推导其极限定理，同时为量子耗尽等具体物理量的统计提供可控的定量结果。

Method: 核心方法是给出粒子数的随机变量表示：将粒子数分解为无穷多个独立几何分布随机变量之和。基于该表示，结合概率极限理论（大数/小数定律、大偏差原理、中心极限定理）分别处理晶格间距发散与趋于零两种尺度极限；并通过与相互作用势散射长度的显式关系将结果应用于Bose--Einstein凝聚的量子耗尽。

Result: (1) 证实了物理文献中关于粒子数可表示为独立几何随机变量无穷和之预测；(2) 得到粒子数的指数尾部界；(3) 当晶格间距发散时，得到小数定律（law of small numbers）及对数速度的大偏差原理；(4) 当晶格间距趋于零时，得到中心极限定理；(5) 作为应用，给出了Bose--Einstein凝聚中量子耗尽统计的细致描述，且常数以相互作用势的散射长度显式表达。

Conclusion: 局部相互作用玻色准自由态的粒子数具有普适的几何分解结构，该结构统一地给出尾部界与多尺度极限定理，并为量子耗尽统计提供了显式的定量描述。这一结果为相关物理预测提供了严格数学基础。

Abstract: We study the particle number distribution of locally interacting bosonic quasi- free states, which arise in various areas of mathematical physics. We show that the particle number decomposes into an infinite sum of independent geometrically distributed random variables, confirming predictions from the physics literature. This representation yields exponential tail bounds for the particle number, a law of small numbers together with a large deviation principle at logarithmic speed when the lattice spacing diverges, and a central limit theorem when it vanishes. As an application, we obtain a detailed description of the statistics of the quantum depletion in Bose--Einstein condensates, with the constants explicit in terms of the scattering length of the interaction potential.

</details>


### [145] [Why we should condition denoising diffusion generative models on windows of past observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10924)
*Matthias Morzfeld,Daniel Hodyss*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 将扩散模型条件化于短窗口的过去观测，可在不重新训练的前提下达到循环DA/DOP系统的最小后验误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据同化（DA）是循环过程，依赖随时间变化的先验把过去观测的信息传递到未来循环；而扩散生成模型的DA通常基于固定训练集，先验是静态的，忽略了过去观测，导致后验误差大于循环DA系统。但把随时间变化的先验纳入生成模型需要频繁且昂贵的重新训练。受线性系统理论中"卡尔曼滤波预测对过去观测的依赖呈指数衰减"这一性质启发，作者试图用短窗口的过去观测来条件化扩散模型，以在避免重训练的同时保留时序先验信息。

Method: 提出两种训练框架：(1) 扩散DA系统，在给定一组过去观测的条件下预测当前状态；(2) 扩散"直接观测预测"（DOP）系统，在给定一组过去观测的条件下预测未来观测。并在一个典型线性系统上进行验证，利用其解析可处理性排除神经网络训练误差的干扰。

Result: 在该线性系统上，两种系统均能达到完全循环DA/DOP系统所具备的最小后验误差，且无需重新训练，只要时间窗口足够长。同时确认：对过去观测窗口进行条件化，是实现高效且准确的基于扩散的DA或DOP的必要条件。

Conclusion: 以短窗口过去观测条件化扩散模型，可有效替代昂贵的时序先验重训练，在线性设定下达到循环DA/DOP的最优后验精度。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) is, traditionally, a cycling process that relies on time-dependent priors to propagate information from past observations to future cycles. Using denoising diffusion generative modeling for DA is challenging because standard approaches use a fixed training data set, which in turn leads to a static prior that ignores information from past observations. Because past observations are ignored, DA systems with static priors lead to larger posterior errors than cycling DA systems. Incorporating time-dependent priors into generative models, however, requires expensive and frequent retraining. Motivated by linear systems theory - where the dependence of a prediction of a Kalman filter on past observations decays exponentially - we condition diffusion models on short windows of past observations. Specifically, we describe training procedures for two frameworks: a diffusion DA system predicting the current state given a set of past observations, and a diffusion ``direct observation prediction'' (DOP) system, predicting future observations given a set of past observations. Using a canonical linear system, we show that both systems can achieve the minimal posterior error characteristic of a fully-cycled DA/DOP system, without re-training, provided the time windows are long enough. The linear setup ensures analytical tractability, avoids confounding neural network training errors, and confirms that conditioning on windows of past observations is required for efficient and accurate diffusion-based DA or DOP.

</details>


### [146] [The kinematic structures and the inertial geometry of a moving charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11165)
*César S. López-Monsalvo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文探究带电粒子的运动在多大程度上决定了其所在几何、又在多大程度上依赖粒子自身带来的结构。作者仅要求辛结构像Hamilton方程那样联系速度与动量，并对所有能量同时成立，证明满足该要求的结构正是典范辛结构及其被底空间闭2-形式的"扭转"；场提供2-形式，粒子提供其前的乘子，并将该乘子本原地等同于电荷。进一步地，粒子获得动量的映射的度决定了场进入Newton第二定律的度：反对称双线性形式不产生Lorentz力，而Randers度量产生一个长度，其与Riemann度量的差在速度中线性；该度量已内含于扭转结构，作为自由能某一水平上的原函数，其输运律为非线性，且无已知仿射联络可胜任。在不定号情形下，长度部分与动力学分离，极值线由最短转为最长。在圆球上，单极通量不留下此类度量，但输运仍存，且给定单位作用量的预量子化将电荷限制在格点上。结论是每个荷质比获得各自独有的几何，故按功能主义标准，没有一个是带电粒子的时空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者追问：带电粒子所运动于其中的几何，有多少是被其运动所固定的，又有多少必须由粒子本身携带。传统做法预先假定一个固定的时空/辛结构，本文则反过来，仅以Hamilton方程所要求的"速度—动量"关系为约束，试图从运动本身反推出允许的几何结构及其与粒子属性（电荷、质量）的分配关系。

Method: 作者仅要求辛结构满足Hamilton方程式的速度—动量关系，且要求该关系对每一个能量同时成立。在此约束下分类满足条件的辛结构，得到典范辛结构及其被底空间闭2-形式的扭转。将2-形式解释为场、其前乘子解释为粒子的电荷。继而考察粒子获得动量的映射的度，并据此分析场进入Newton第二定律的度，比较反对称双线性形式与Randers度量的情形。进一步把该度量定位为扭转结构中自由能某一水平上的原函数，研究其非线性输运律、不定号情形下的极值行为，以及在圆球上与单极通量、预量子化和电荷格点化的关系。

Result: 满足"对所有能量同时维系速度—动量关系"的辛结构恰为典范结构及其被底空间闭2-形式的扭转。场给出2-形式，粒子给出其乘子，并本原地等同于电荷。映射的度决定场进入Newton第二定律的度：反对称双线性形式不产生Lorentz力，Randers度量产生Lorentz力，其长度与Riemann长度之差在速度中线性。该度量已存在于扭转结构中，是自由能某一水平上的原函数，其输运律非线性、无已知仿射联络适用。不定号下长度与动力学分离，极值线由最短变为最长。圆球上单极通量不生成此类度量，但输运仍在，预量子化（给定单位作用量）将电荷限制为格点。

Conclusion: 每个荷质比对应一种自身独有的几何，因此按功能主义判据，没有任何单一的几何（包括通常意义上的时空）能作为带电粒子的时空。几何的一部分由运动固定，另一部分由粒子携带；电荷与质量的比值决定了粒子所经历的几何。

Abstract: We ask how much of the geometry a charged particle moves in is fixed by its motion, and how much a particle must bring. We ask of a symplectic structure only that it relate velocity to momentum as Hamilton's equations do, and we ask it of every energy at once. In particular, we show that the structures meeting that demand are the canonical one and its twists by a closed two-form of the base. A field provides the two-form, a particle the multiplier before it, which we identify constitutively with its charge. Thus, a single energy governs a family of structures, and each particle takes the one its charge fixes. We then ask what a particle must bring to be given a momentum, and we show that the degree of that map settles the degree at which a field enters Newton's Second Law. An antisymmetric bilinear form returns no Lorentz force, whilst a Randers metric returns one --- a length whose difference from a Riemannian one is linear in the velocity. Moreover, we find that metric already within the twisted structure, as its primitive over a level of the free energy, and its law of transport to be nonlinear, no affine connection being known to serve. Under an indefinite signature the length parts from the dynamics, and the extremals turn from shortest to longest. On the round sphere a monopole flux leaves no such metric, whilst the transport remains and prequantisation, given a unit of action, restricts the charge to a lattice. In this manner, we conclude that each charge-to-mass ratio receives a geometry of its own, so that by a functionalist criterion none of them is the spacetime of a charged particle.

</details>


### [147] [Recent Advances on a Model for the Electromagnetic Dirac Field at Thermal Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11283)
*Umberto Morellini*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 综述正温度下相对论电子-正电子场与经典电磁场相互作用模型的近期进展，目标是理解热背景对量子系统极化机制的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需推进对热背景如何影响该量子系统极化机制的理解。

Method: 回顾正温度下相对论电子-正电子场与经典电磁场相互作用模型的近期研究进展。

Result: 属综述性工作，汇总该模型近期进展，未报告新实验/数值结果。

Conclusion: 该综述旨在深化对热背景影响量子系统极化机制的认识。

Abstract: We review recent progress on a model for the relativistic electron-positron field interacting with a classical electromagnetic field at positive temperature. The ultimate goal is to advance our comprehension of how thermal backgrounds affect polarisation mechanisms of this quantum system.

</details>


### [148] [A proof of the Hodge universality conjecture at nonzero dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11469)
*Francisco Hernández Iglesias*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 证明了标量tau-对称哈密顿形变在广义标准形式下由首个哈密顿密度的特定系数唯一确定，并结合Buryak–Rossi的构造，建立非零色散下的Hodge普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画Riemann hierarchy的标量tau-对称哈密顿形变的唯一性，以确定何种形变数据能唯一决定原层级，并为Hodge普适性提供非零色散情形的理论基础。

Method: 研究广义标准形式下的标量tau-对称哈密顿形变；证明其由首个哈密顿密度中$u_x^2$的非零系数及$u_{xx}^g$（$g\ge2$）的系数唯一确定；再结合Buryak–Rossi关于Hodge类double ramification hierarchies的构造。

Result: 该形变被唯一确定，且原层级在normal Miura变换意义下被确定；据此建立了非零色散下的Hodge普适性。

Conclusion: Riemann hierarchy的标量tau-对称哈密顿形变具有由少数低阶项系数决定的刚性，进而得到Hodge普适性在非零色散情形下的成立。

Abstract: We prove that a scalar tau-symmetric Hamiltonian deformation of the Riemann hierarchy in generalized standard form is uniquely determined by the nonzero coefficient of $u_x^2$ and the coefficients of $u_{xx}^g$, $g\ge2$, in its first Hamiltonian density. This determines the original hierarchy up to normal Miura transformations. Combined with the construction of Hodge-class double ramification hierarchies by Buryak--Rossi, this establishes Hodge universality at nonzero dispersion.

</details>


### [149] [On asymptotic expansions of the density of states for Poisson distributed random Schrödinger operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11603)
*Jan Fischer,David Hasler,Jannis Koberstein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究泊松过程随机 Schrödinger 算符，在弱无序极限下推导预解式迹的渐近展开，给出展开系数显式估计，证明其无穷体积极限在谱参数趋于自由 Laplacian 谱时有限，并应用于积分态密度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机 Schrödinger 算符的弱无序极限是数学物理中的重要问题，预解式迹的渐近展开可用于刻画谱性质，但缺乏系数的显式估计及无穷体积极限的有限性分析。

Method: 考虑势场服从泊松过程的随机 Schrödinger 算符；在弱无序（小扰动）极限下对预解式的迹进行渐近展开，显式估计各阶展开系数，并分析当谱参数逼近自由 Laplacian 谱时系数的无穷体积极限。

Result: 得到预解式迹的渐近展开及系数的显式估计；证明展开系数的无穷体积极限在谱参数趋于自由 Laplacian 谱时保持有限。

Conclusion: 作为应用，由上述展开与估计导出了积分态密度的界。

Abstract: We study a random Schrödinger operator with a potential distributed according to a Poisson process. Asymptotic expansions for traces of resolvents in the limit of small disorder are derived. Explicit estimates for the expansion coefficients are given and we show that their infinite volume limits are finite as the spectral parameter approaches the spectrum of the free Laplacian. As an application we derive bounds on the integrated density of states.

</details>


### [150] [A closed signature formula for the Katz-Long-Moody Hermitian form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11793)
*Haru Negami*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了Katz-Long-Moody构造在酉输入下所得商表示上不变Hermitian形式的符号差闭式公式，精确判定其正定性，并应用于Pochhammer系统、Beukers-Heckman交错准则及Hecke与Temperley-Lieb特化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Katz-Long-Moody构造对自由群与辫群半直积的表示给出新表示，其在纯辫群上对应Haraoka的乘法中间卷积；此前伴随论文提出了该形式何时正定的问题，本文旨在解决该正定性问题并给出符号差的精确刻画。

Method: 证明仅使用初等线性代数：一个行列式恒等式、显式块主元公式，以及关于酉矩阵Cayley变换之和的惯性公式。针对酉输入及单位圆上非1的卷积参数，对商表示赋予典范非退化不变Hermitian形式，并用输入局部单值群的特征角、其有序乘积的特征角及卷积参数给出符号差闭式公式。

Result: 得到符号差的闭式公式，涵盖过渡到商之前的核以及共振参数处的符号差变化；精确判定诱导形式何时正定。正定蕴含输出表示可酉化，且在输出表示不可约时反之亦成立。

Conclusion: 解决了伴随论文提出的正定性问题：正定性与输出表示的可酉化密切相关。应用方面，显式比较了秩一构造与Haraoka对Pochhammer系统的不变形式，恢复了Beukers-Heckman交错准则的Gauss情形，并确定了Hecke与Temperley-Lieb特化的正定参数区间。

Abstract: The Katz-Long-Moody construction associates to a representation of the semidirect product of a free group and a braid group, defined by the Artin action, and a nonzero parameter a new representation of the same group. On the pure braid group it corresponds to Haraoka's multiplicative middle convolution for KZ-type equations. For unitary input and a convolution parameter on the unit circle other than one, the construction equips the quotient representation with a canonical non-degenerate invariant Hermitian form. We give a closed formula for its signature in terms of the eigenangles of the input local monodromies, the eigenangles of their ordered product, and the convolution parameter. The formula accounts for the kernel of the form before passage to the quotient and for signature changes at resonant parameters. It determines precisely when the induced form is definite, answering the definiteness problem posed in the companion paper. Definiteness implies unitarizability of the output representation; the converse holds when that representation is irreducible.
  The proof uses elementary linear algebra: a determinant identity, explicit block-pivot formulas, and an inertia formula for sums of Cayley transforms of unitary matrices. As applications, we compare the rank-one construction explicitly with Haraoka's invariant form for Pochhammer systems, recover the Gauss case of the Beukers-Heckman interlacing criterion, and determine the definite parameter intervals for the Hecke and Temperley-Lieb specializations.

</details>

### [151] [Homogeneous Milnor fibers and Kato--Matsumoto bounds via simplicial multiwedges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11396)
*Masaharu Ishikawa,Tat-Thang Nguyen*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 对任意 $n\geq 3$、$s\geq 2$，构造出次数为 $n(n+1)/2$ 的齐次多项式，其 Milnor 纤维恰好 $2s$-连通且非形式，并达到 Kato–Matsumoto 连通性上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答 Suciu 提出的两个问题，并说明在达到 Kato–Matsumoto 连通性上界的 Milnor 纤维中可以出现非平凡的 $n$ 重 Massey 积，从而破坏形式性。

Method: 基于 Limonchenko 引入的简单多面体神经复形的单形多楔构造，结合 Suciu 关于加权齐次 Milnor 纤维的实现方法，构造特定次数的齐次多项式。

Result: 所得 Milnor 纤维恰好为 $2s$-连通，其有理上同调在 $2s+1$ 次类上含严格定义的非平凡 $n$ 重 Massey 积，因此该纤维非形式，同时达到 Kato–Matsumoto 连通性上界。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了达到连通性上界却非形式的 Milnor 纤维实例，回答了 Suciu 的两个问题。

Abstract: For every $n\geq 3$ and $s\geq 2$, we construct a homogeneous polynomial of degree $n(n+1)/2$ whose Milnor fiber is exactly $2s$-connected and whose rational cohomology contains a strictly defined nontrivial $n$-fold Massey product on classes of degree $2s+1$, implying that the Milnor fiber is non-formal, while attaining the Kato--Matsumoto connectivity bound. Our construction is based on the simplicial multiwedges of the nerve complexes of simple polytopes introduced by Limonchenko, combined with Suciu's realization of weighted homogeneous Milnor fibers. We thereby answer two problems posed by Suciu.

</details>


### [152] [The homotopy types of directed path and trace spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11454)
*Philippe Gaucher*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 构造了一个饱和有向空间，其底空间为Hausdorff的Δ-生成空间，其中两点间的迹空间同胚于正方形，而有向路径空间具非平凡基本群，故规范商映射非弱同伦等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有向拓扑学中，迹空间（trace space）与有向路径空间之间的关系是刻画有向同伦论的核心问题。通常期望规范商映射（从有向路径空间到迹空间）是弱同伦等价，从而可用迹空间替代路径空间进行同伦计算。本文旨在检验这一期望是否普遍成立，寻找反例。

Method: 构造一个饱和有向空间，其余底空间为Hausdorff的Δ-生成空间，并选取两个不同点，使它们之间的迹空间同胚于正方形；同时对有向路径空间（以及正则有向路径模去递增同胚后的空间）的基本群进行计算与分析。

Result: 所得空间中，两点间迹空间同胚于正方形（因而可缩、基本群平凡），但有向路径空间具有非平凡基本群。因此从有向路径空间到迹空间的规范商映射不是弱同伦等价。对正则有向路径模去递增同胚的商空间，同样得到非平凡基本群与相同结论。

Conclusion: 该反例表明，即便在底空间为Hausdorff且Δ-生成的饱和有向空间中，迹空间与有向路径空间也可能不具有相同的弱同伦型，规范商映射一般不是弱同伦等价；对正则有向路径模递增同胚的情形同样成立。

Abstract: We construct a saturated directed space with a Hausdorff $Δ$-generated underlying space and two distinct points such that the trace space between them is homeomorphic to a square, whereas the directed path space has a nontrivial fundamental group. In particular, the canonical quotient map is not a weak homotopy equivalence. The same conclusion holds for regular directed paths modulo increasing homeomorphisms.

</details>


### [153] [Moduli spaces of geometric functorial field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11812)
*Jacek Kenig,Dmitri Pavlov*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该文构建工具，将几何函子场论的模空间刻画为等变单纯预层之间的映射空间，并定义几何结构的笛卡尔实现，用其给出模空间的映射空间表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为计算几何函子场论的模空间，需要一套合适的范畴/同伦论框架，将几何结构与场论模空间统一表述。

Method: 对光滑 d-流形族站点上的预层所表示的 d 维几何结构 F，定义其笛卡尔实现——Cartesian 空间站点上的 O(d)-等变单纯预层；并利用笛卡尔实现，将具有几何结构 F 的函子场论模空间表示为 O(d)-等变单纯预层之间的映射空间。

Result: 给出了以 O(d)-等变单纯预层映射空间表示几何函子场论模空间的构造性结果。

Conclusion: 该框架为几何函子场论模空间的计算提供工具，并在姊妹篇中用于计算取值于任意光滑对称幺半无穷范畴的一维光滑定向黎曼函子场论模空间。

Abstract: We develop tools to compute moduli spaces of geometric functorial field theories as mapping spaces of equivariant simplicial presheaves. Given a d-dimensional geometric structure F, presented as a presheaf on the site of smooth families of d-manifolds, we define its Cartesian realization, which is an O(d)-equivariant simplicial presheaf on the site of Cartesian spaces. We use Cartesian realizations to present the moduli space of functorial field theories with geometric structure F as a mapping space between O(d)-equivariant simplicial presheaves. In a companion paper, we use this result to compute the moduli space of smooth one-dimensional oriented Riemannian functorial field theories valued in an arbitrary smooth symmetric monoidal infinity-category.

</details>


### [154] [Cusp restrictions and Bunke--Naumann invariants with level structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11924)
*Yuqi Li,Hao-Yu Sun*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该文研究模曲线尖点除子上二次不变量，证明对四整除权重的整弱全纯一级模形式 $h$，其导入值 $[h/2]$ 在每个非平凡 $\Gamma_0(N)$ 层消失，并刻画奇数素层尖点谱及 level 3 的周期 $\nu$ 族探测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 限制到模曲线的完全尖点除子会产生一个二次不变量，其有理不确定性来自单个整体模形式；作者希望精确控制该不变量在各层 $\Gamma_0(N)$ 上的消失性与整性，并连接到同伦理论中的高阶不变量与消灭现象。

Method: 先在完备化前局部化系数，再在过渡到同伦群之后进行有理化；在奇数素层将尖点谱识别为两个实 Tate 因子的乘积并构造单个全纯权二修正；利用 Mahowald–Rezk 同伦计算与尖点留数同态探测二次值；通过反转 2 把等式从偶数层推广。

Result: 对任意四整除权重的整弱全纯一级模形式 $h$，$[h/2]$ 在每个非平凡 $\Gamma_0(N)$ 层消失；奇数素层给出两实 Tate 因子乘积的完整尖点谱及权二修正，产生整整体同伦类与有理整体源类满足联合消灭等式；该等式传递到所有奇数合数层，偶数层经反转 2 得到；level 3 中周期 $\nu$ 族在 $8K+3$ 茎上的二次值恰为二阶；应用于双次数 $(8k+1,8k'+2)$ 乘积的 Bunke–Naumann 二次不变量在各非平凡层消失。

Conclusion: 尖点除子限制的二次不变量的有理不确定性可由单个整体模形式控制，且其导入值在全部非平凡层消失；构造的全纯修正与联合消灭等式在奇偶层均成立，并为 Bunke–Naumann 二次不变量的消灭提供框架。

Abstract: Restriction to the full cusp divisor of a modular curve defines a secondary invariant whose rational indeterminacy comes from a single global modular form. For every integral weakly holomorphic level-one modular form $h$ of weight divisible by four, we prove that the imported value $[h/2]$ vanishes at every nontrivial $Γ_0(N)$ level. The construction localizes coefficients before completion and rationalizes only after passing to homotopy groups. At odd prime level, we identify the full cusp spectrum as a product of two real Tate factors and construct a single holomorphic weight-two correction. This correction yields an actual integral global homotopy class and a rational global source class satisfying the equality required for joint annihilation. The equality transports to every odd composite level, while even levels follow by inverting two. At level three, the Mahowald--Rezk homotopy calculation leaves only the periodic $ν$ family in stems $8K+3$. A cusp-residue homomorphism detects its secondary value, which has exact order two throughout the periodic family. As an application, the Bunke--Naumann secondary invariants of products in bidegrees $(8k+1,8k'+2)$ vanish after passage to every nontrivial level.

</details>

### [155] [Vectorial lattice Boltzmann solver for compressible inviscid flows with generic equation of state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10639)
*S. A. Hosseini,I. V. Karlin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带时空自适应松弛系数的矢量格子Boltzmann模型，结合自适应时间步进，用于求解具有一般状态方程的可压缩Euler方程，并在数值算例中验证了其精度与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可压缩气体动力学（尤其是非理想状态方程，如BZT区域）的数值模拟对格式的稳健性和精度要求较高，而传统格子Boltzmann方法在处理一般状态方程与激波问题时存在局限，因此需要一个兼具高阶精度与强稳健性的求解器。

Method: 构建了矢量格子Boltzmann模型，引入时空自适应松弛系数，并配合自适应时间步进策略；该松弛系数的特殊形式使模型在声学标度下、无激波情形中收敛到Euler极限且具有二阶精度。

Result: 该求解器稳健，能正确捕捉理想与非理想状态方程下的可压缩气体动力学行为，包括Bethe–Zel'dovich–Thompson区域，并通过一系列复杂度递增的算例得到验证与展示。

Conclusion: 所提自适应矢量格子Boltzmann方法是一种适用于一般状态方程可压缩Euler方程的高精度、强稳健求解方案，可有效处理非理想气体及BZT等复杂流动情形。

Abstract: We develop a vectorial lattice Boltzmann model with a space-time adaptive relaxation coefficient, combined with adaptive time-stepping for compressible Euler dynamics with generic equation of state. The model, due to special form of the relaxation coefficient devised here is shown to converge to the Euler limit with second-order accuracy in the absence of shocks under acoustic scaling. The solver is robust and able to properly capture compressible gas dynamics for both ideal and non-ideal equations of state, including the Bethe--Zel'dovich--Thompson regime. This is verified and demonstrated through a variety of configurations of increasing complexity.

</details>


### [156] [Structure-preserving operator splitting for 2.5D ideal MHD with an entropy-stable DGSEM and exactly divergence-free compatible finite elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10849)
*Siyuan Fan,Guosheng Fu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种用于2.5D理想磁流体力学的高阶全显式算子分裂方法，在笛卡尔网格上耦合熵稳定DG谱元法与相容有限元磁-速度子流求解器，并经二阶Strang分裂组合，具备保正、保熵稳定、无散度等性质，通过多类基准算例验证了精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对2.5D理想磁流体力学，需要在笛卡尔网格上同时获得高阶精度、显式时间推进，并在出现激波、剪切等非光滑流动时保持数值稳定（正性、熵稳定性）以及磁场无散度约束。现有方法往往难以兼顾高阶精度与非线性稳定性。

Method: 采用算子分裂框架：流体子流用熵稳定间断伽辽金谱元法（DG-SEM），配以逐级振荡消除与保正限制器；磁-速度子流采用相容有限元，并对电场与电流密度作质量集中重构，其离散旋度更新精确保持全局H(div)无散子空间，理想半离散格式使动能、磁能与内能平衡。引入旋度型人工电阻率与方向分辨速度滤波，将耗散掉的磁能与动能返还内能。两求解器以二阶流体-磁-流体Strang分裂组合，形成无矩阵方案。

Result: 光滑Alfvén波与被平流涡算例显示磁多项式阶数呈奇偶收敛模式，验证了时间二阶精度，且稳定化不破坏高阶精度。场环、Orszag-Tang、转子、MHD爆炸波与Kelvin-Helmholtz等算例表明对非光滑多维流动性能稳健，人工电阻率可抑制网格尺度磁振荡同时保留可分辨结构。

Conclusion: 该方法在保证质量守恒、节点可取性与磁场无散度（在接受的步上）的同时，实现了高阶、显式、鲁棒且稳定的2.5D理想MHD模拟。

Abstract: We develop a high-order, fully explicit operator-splitting method for 2.5D ideal magnetohydrodynamics on Cartesian meshes. The hydrodynamic subflow is advanced by an entropy-stable discontinuous Galerkin spectral element method equipped with stagewise oscillation elimination and a positivity-preserving limiter. The magnetic--velocity subflow uses compatible finite elements and mass-lumped reconstructions of electric field and current density. Its discrete-curl update exactly preserves the global $H(\mathrm{div})$ divergence-free subspace, while the ideal semidiscretization balances kinetic, magnetic, and internal energy. Curl-form artificial resistivity and a direction-resolved velocity filter return removed magnetic and kinetic energy to internal energy; consequently, the stabilized magnetic stage preserves positive internal energy and satisfies a discrete entropy inequality. The two solvers are composed by a second-order hydrodynamic--magnetic--hydrodynamic Strang splitting, yielding a matrix-free scheme that retains global mass, nodal admissibility, and magnetic divergence on accepted steps. Smooth Alfvén-wave and advected-vortex tests reveal an even--odd convergence pattern in the magnetic polynomial degree, verify second-order temporal accuracy, and show that stabilization preserves high-order accuracy. Field-loop, Orszag--Tang, rotor, MHD blast-wave, and Kelvin--Helmholtz calculations demonstrate robust performance for nonsmooth multidimensional flows and show that artificial resistivity suppresses grid-scale magnetic oscillations while retaining the resolved structures.

</details>


### [157] [Quadrature by Matched Asymptotic Expansion (QBMAX): Accelerating Evaluation of Layer Potentials with Boundary Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10857)
*Chaoqi Lin,Xiaoyu Wei,Andreas Klöckner*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对大虚波数Helmholtz层势中快速衰减核与边界层导致的数值困难，提出高阶QBMAX方法，将核的渐近衰减行为融入势的Taylor展开，在2D/3D算例中精度较QBX最高提升四位数字。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速衰减核的层势在存在边界层时（如大虚波数的Helmholtz层势）难以高精度计算。传统QBX用与核无关的Taylor展开逼近势，未能利用核的渐近衰减特性，导致在强衰减、大参数情形下精度与收敛性下降。

Method: 提出Quadrature by Matched Asymptotic Expansion（QBMAX），在QBX框架基础上，将核的渐近衰减行为直接嵌入势的Taylor展开中，构造匹配渐近展开型局部展开核；通过符号化的加权运算量模型比较两类展开核的构造成本，并在2D与3D数值实验中与QBX对比。

Result: 在测试的大参数情形下，2D与3D数值结果较QBX最高提升约四位数字精度；在较小参数或曲率更强的几何上两者表现相近。符号加权运算量模型显示两种展开核的形成成本相当；Taylor展开形式阶数相同，而QBMAX在平直边界模型中降低了截断误差常数，且在实验报告中普遍表现出更弱的收敛退化。

Conclusion: QBMAX以可比的构造成本实现与QBX同等的形式阶数，并在大参数、含边界层的问题中显著提升精度、减小截断误差常数与收敛退化，是对QBX框架的有效扩展，适用于快速衰减核层势的高阶求积。

Abstract: Evaluating layer potentials with rapidly decaying kernels becomes computationally challenging in the presence of boundary layers, as occurs for Helmholtz layer potentials with large imaginary wavenumbers. We introduce Quadrature by Matched Asymptotic Expansion (QBMAX), a high-order method that extends the Quadrature by Expansion (QBX) framework by incorporating the kernel's asymptotic decay behavior directly into the Taylor expansion of the potential. Numerical results in both 2D and 3D show improvements of up to four digits over QBX for the tested large-parameter cases, while the methods can perform similarly for smaller parameters or more strongly curved geometries. A symbolic weighted operation-count model indicates comparable costs for forming the two expansion kernels. The Taylor expansions have the same formal order, while QBMAX reduces the truncation-error constants in the flat-boundary model and generally exhibits less convergence degradation in the reported experiments.

</details>


### [158] [Preserving Structure in Variational Data Assimilation of Hyperbolic Conservation Laws via Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10906)
*Yao Xiao,Anne Gelb*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对双曲守恒律的变分资料同化，提出基于稀疏促进残差变换的新型正则项，通过广义稀疏贝叶斯学习求解非凸目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲守恒律的变分资料同化面临困难：高斯假设强制平滑，会抹平跳跃间断。稀疏促进正则项可引入结构先验缓解该问题，但需谨慎选择先验，因为它既要抵消结构误设的背景误差协方差（在平滑区引入伪振荡），又要应对空间稀疏观测（数据不足无法约束状态）。此外，守恒律状态变量通常不具有标准稀疏算子（如全变差）所假设的分段常数结构，其解的"变异性"既预先未知也不固定，故高阶全变差同样不适用。

Method: 提出一种新的资料同化正则项，建立在稀疏促进残差变换之上，既不固定平滑阶数，也不要求预先知道潜在变异性。在三维持变分框架内，用广义稀疏贝叶斯学习求解所得非凸目标：将全局 L1 惩罚替换为残差变换上的层次条件高斯先验，通过学习的超先验为每个空间点赋予位置特定的权重。

Result: 在标量、浅水及 Euler 测试问题上的数值实验表明：该正则项比全变差更准确地解析间断，且当背景误差协方差误设程度增加、观测变得更稀疏时，其优势进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 所提基于残差变换的稀疏促进正则项能有效改善双曲守恒律资料同化中对间断的解析能力，且在背景误差协方差误设和观测稀疏的情形下鲁棒性优于全变差方法。

Abstract: Hyperbolic conservation laws pose a challenging setting for variational data assimilation, since the Gaussian assumption imposes a smoothness that smears jump discontinuities. A sparsity promoting regularization can help to mitigate this problem by incorporating a structural prior into the variational objective. Care must be taken when choosing this prior because it serves to counterbalance both a structurally misspecified background-error covariance, which introduces spurious oscillations over smooth regions, as well as spatially sparse observations, which leave too little data to constrain the state. Moreover, state variables of hyperbolic conservation laws typically do not have piecewise constant structure, which is an assumption made when using standard sparsity-promoting operators, such as total variation. Indeed the underlying {\em variability} of hyperbolic conservation law solutions are neither known in advance nor fixed. Hence using higher order total variation is also not suitable. Here we introduce a new regularization term for data assimilation, built on the sparsity promoting residual transform that neither commits to a fixed smoothness order nor requires the underlying variability to be known a priori. We solve the resulting nonconvex objective within a three-dimensional variational framework using generalized sparse Bayesian learning. This approach replaces a global \(\ell _{1}\) penalty with a hierarchical conditional Gaussian prior on the residual transform, where a learned hyper-prior yields location-specific weights attached to each spatial point. Numerical experiments across scalar, shallow-water, and Euler test problems show that this regularization term resolves discontinuities more accurately than total variation, and that its advantage grows as the background-error covariance becomes misspecified and the observations become sparser.

</details>


### [159] [A virtual element method for Kelvin--Voigt viscoelasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10908)
*Utkarsh Rajput,Ankit Kumar,Andres E. Rubiano,Ricardo Ruiz-Baier,Sarvesh Kumar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文采用协调虚拟元方法（VEM）结合二阶Crank-Nicolson时间离散，数值求解Kelvin-Voigt粘弹性模型，建立半离散与全离散问题的适定性并给出先验误差估计，通过数值算例验证理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟元方法具有计算优势，可用于粘弹性问题的数值逼近，然而Kelvin-Voigt粘弹性模型的协调虚拟元离散及误差分析尚需系统研究。

Method: 针对原始（primal）弱形式，空间上采用协调虚拟元方法离散，时间上采用二阶Crank-Nicolson格式离散，构建半离散与全离散格式，并进行适定性分析与先验误差估计。

Result: 证明了半离散与全离散问题的适定性，推导了先验误差估计，并通过若干代表性数值算例验证了理论结果，展示了该格式的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的协调VEM结合二阶Crank-Nicolson格式是求解Kelvin-Voigt粘弹性模型的有效数值方法，理论与数值结果一致。

Abstract: Considering the computational advantages of virtual element methods (VEM), this work employs a conforming VEM for the numerical approximation of the Kelvin--Voigt model of viscoelasticity. For clarity and simplicity, we focus on the primal formulation. The spatial discretization is carried out using the virtual element method, while the temporal discretization is handled via the {second-order Crank--Nicolson} scheme. We establish the well-posedness of both the semi-discrete and fully discrete problems and derive {\it a priori} error estimates. Several representative numerical examples are presented to validate the theoretical results and to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed formulation.

</details>


### [160] [Stress-divergence, Laplacian, and rotational forms of the incompressible Navier--Stokes equations with variable viscosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10919)
*Benjamin Cisternas,Douglas R. Q. Pacheco,Felipe Galarce,Ernesto Castillo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文比较Navier–Stokes方程中三种粘性项表述（对称梯度、广义Laplacian、广义旋转形式）在变粘性及非牛顿/热/湍流模型下的实现、效率、数值稳定性与出流边界条件差异，采用二阶IMEX时间离散并进行稳定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可压缩性允许将粘性项改写为多种形式，产生不同数值性质；而变粘性模型（非牛顿、热、湍流）打破常粘性假设并引入额外一致性项，因此需要系统比较不同粘性表述的差异。

Method: 比较对称梯度扩散项与变粘性推广的Laplacian及旋转形式；针对时间依赖流动采用二阶隐式-显式（IMEX）时间离散；通过严格稳定性分析考察特定显式处理如何规避算法非线性而不引入CFL条件；并进行数值测试。

Result: 稳定性分析表明选定的显式处理可绕过算法非线性且不引发CFL条件；数值结果揭示三种粘性表述存在重要差异，尤其在出流边界处，广义Laplacian形式在扩散主导区域更适用。

Conclusion: 三种粘性表述在实现、效率、稳定性及出流边界条件上存在显著差异；广义Laplacian形式在扩散主导且存在出流边界时更具优势，为变粘性流动的数值格式选择提供依据。

Abstract: In the Navier--Stokes equations, incompressibility allows rewriting the viscous term in various forms leading to distinct numerical properties and flow descriptions. Furthermore, models accounting for non-Newtonian, thermal or turbulent effects often break the constant-viscosity assumption, thereby producing additional consistency terms. In this context, the present work compares the classical symmetric-gradient diffusion term with more recent variable-viscosity generalizations of the Laplacian and rotational forms. We discuss, analyze and test their differences with respect to implementation, efficiency, numerical stability and outflow boundary conditions. With a focus on time-dependent flows, we consider second-order implicit-explicit (IMEX) temporal discretizations aimed at improving efficiency and numerical stability. Through a rigorous stability analysis, we show how selected explicit treatments can bypass algorithmic nonlinearities without inducing CFL conditions. Our numerical results highlight important differences between the three viscous formulations---especially in the presence of outflow boundaries, for which the generalized Laplacian form proves more suitable in diffusion-dominated regimes. %(as widely known for constant viscosity).

</details>


### [161] [Full-window branch discovery and loss-selected EnKF continuation for data assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10949)
*Angxiu Ni*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文提出离线全窗口分支发现框架（可选接在线EnKF续算）。核心机制包括：伴随路径核（APK）微分、优化高斯初始律、损失加权混合。40维Lorenz-96实验中，APK离线路径RMSE较群体弱4D-Var_x小4.3倍，APK-EnKF在线RMSE较普通EnKF小64倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对数据同化/路径优化中非线性系统易陷入局部极小、探索与利用难以平衡、初始态盆地搜索不充分等问题，作者希望构建一个能离线发现多分支路径并可无缝衔接到在线滤波的框架。

Method: 提出离线全窗口分支发现框架，含三大机制：(1) 伴随路径核（APK）微分，平衡核微分与校正稳定化的路径扰动，使优化从探索转向利用；(2) 优化高斯初始律，扩大初始态盆地搜索范围；(3) 跨独立运行的损失加权混合，重组成功路径分量。随后用局部损失选取内部状态，并以EnKF继续在线同化（APK-EnKF）。

Result: 在40维Lorenz-96实验中：APK的平均离线路径RMSE比群体弱4D-Var_x小4.3倍；所得APK-EnKF方法的平均在线RMSE比普通EnKF小64倍。

Conclusion: 该框架有效提升了离线路径发现的精度并显著改善在线同化性能，验证了APK机制与EnKF结合的优越性。

Abstract: We develop a framework for offline full-window branch discovery, optionally followed by online continuation with an ensemble Kalman filter (EnKF). Three mechanisms drive the branch search: adjoint path-kernel (APK) differentiation balances kernel differentiation and correction-stabilized path perturbation, shifting the optimization from exploration to exploitation; an optimized Gaussian initial law broadens the search over initial-state basins; and loss-weighted mixing across independent runs recombines successful path components. We may then select an interior state using a local loss and continue online with an EnKF. In 40-dimensional Lorenz-96 experiments, the mean offline path RMSE of APK is 4.3 times smaller than that of population weak-$\mathrm{4D\text{-}Var}_x$. The resulting APK-EnKF method has a mean online RMSE 64 times smaller than that of ordinary EnKF.

</details>


### [162] [A variational physics-informed graph neural network for heterogeneous solid mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10983)
*Aashay Rajan Yadav,Amiya Prakash Das,Ratna Kumar Annabattula*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种变分式、无标签的物理信息图神经网络（PI-GNN），将异质材料界面的刚度跳跃交由自适应共形网格离散承载而非试函数，在无惩罚项、无界面权重的前提下以单一离散总势能目标求解，von Mises误差在宽刚度对比范围内低于3.58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质固体中的应力局部化由双材料界面控制：位移场为C^0连续，而面内应力因刚度失配发生跳跃。传统基于坐标的PINN需通过预设正则化宽度或加权界面惩罚来表示该跳跃，导致精度对相间刚度对比变化高度敏感。作者希望摆脱对试函数光滑化与惩罚权重的依赖，获得变分一致、无惩罚的界面表示。

Method: 构建变分、无标签的物理信息图神经网络：在共形自适应网格图上求解，按单元赋予本构行为，将离散总势能作为唯一无权重目标函数最小化，目标中仅出现一阶导数。分段线性单元上的离散能量与有限元Ritz泛函一致。Dirichlet边界条件按构造直接施加，不使用惩罚项、界面权重或预设过渡宽度；小应变弹性与有限应变Neo-Hookean超弹性、二维与三维均采用同一固定架构、优化器与损失。

Result: 在E_inc/E_mat∈[10^-2,10^2]的刚度对比扫描中，von Mises误差保持在3.58%以下，而强形式PINN退化至5.58%；位移误差PI-GNN为0.49%，强形式PINN达7.66%。训练后网络将能量型PINN的σ_xx误差减半（5.01% 对 10.94%）。训练成本比单次有限元求解高出一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: PI-GNN提供了一种变分一致、无惩罚的界面表示方法，适用于参数化代理模型与反演识别，而非替代一次性有限元分析。

Abstract: Stress localization in heterogeneous solids is governed by the bimaterial interface, where the displacement field remains $C^0$-continuous, while in-plane stresses jump due to the stiffness mismatch. Coordinate-based physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) represent this jump via a prescribed regularization width or a weighted interface penalty, making their accuracy sensitive to how phase-contrast changes are handled. This work presents a variational, label-free physics-informed graph neural network (PI-GNN) in which the heterogeneity is carried by the discretization rather than by the trial field. The solver operates on a conforming adaptive mesh graph, assigns constitutive behavior per element, and minimizes the discrete total potential energy as a single unweighted objective in which only first derivatives appear. The discrete energy on piecewise-linear elements coincides with the finite element (FE) Ritz functional. Dirichlet conditions are enforced by construction, with no penalty term, no interface weight, and no prescribed transition width. Using one fixed architecture, optimizer, and loss across small-strain elasticity and finite-strain Neo-Hookean hyperelasticity in two and three dimensions, the von Mises error remains below $3.58\%$ across a stiffness-contrast sweep spanning $(E_{\mathrm{inc}}/E_{\mathrm{mat}}\in[10^{-2},10^{2}])$, where a strong-form PINN degrades to $5.58\%$, and its displacement error reaches $7.66\%$ against $0.49\%$ for the PI-GNN. A trained network halves the ($σ_{xx}$) error of an energy-based PINN ($5.01\%$ versus $10.94\%$). Training cost exceeds a single FE solve by more than an order of magnitude, so the construction is a variationally consistent, penalty-free interface representation for parametric surrogates and inverse identification rather than a replacement for a one-off FE analysis.

</details>


### [163] [A Second-Order Method for Locating Critical Points of Prescribed Index on Energy Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11038)
*Qiang Du,Baoming Shi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种求解指定指数临界点的三次正则化二阶方法，含精确与不精确收敛理论及自适应正则化参数策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维能量景观探索中，极小值（指数0）与过渡态（正指数鞍点）均含关键信息；需一种能定位指定指数临界点的通用计算框架。

Method: 提出三次正则化二阶方法：每步按指定指数更新Hessian特征方向、沿选定不稳定特征方向对梯度与Hessian作反射，并通过求解三次正则化子问题构造下一步；同时引入自适应更新三次正则化参数的策略。

Result: 在特征空间与子问题精确求解时，迭代局部二次收敛至指定指数临界点；在不精确设定下保持局部线性收敛，收敛率取决于计算精度；证明算法不动点恰为指定指数临界点；数值实验验证收敛行为、自适应策略有效性及构建解景观的能力。

Conclusion: 该框架统一处理极小值与过渡态定位，理论保证与自适应机制兼备，适用于高维能量景观探索与解景观构建。

Abstract: Exploring high dimensional energy landscape is a challenging problem in many applications where both ground states and transition states offer important information about the underlying physical systems. To facilitate the computational exploration, we propose a cubic-regularized second-order method for locating critical points of prescribed index, including both energy minima (with an index $0$) and transition states (saddle points with positive index). At each step of the iteration, the proposed method involves several ingredients, such as updating the eigen-directions of the Hessian according to the prescribed index, reflecting the gradient and the Hessian along selected unstable eigen-directions, if any, and constructing the next step by solving a cubic-regularized subproblem. Under suitable assumptions, we show that, when the eigenspace and the cubic subproblem are solved exactly, the iteration converges locally and quadratically to a critical point of the prescribed index. We further establish convergence results in the inexact setting, where both the eigenspace computation and the solution of the cubic subproblem are performed approximately. We show that the resulting iteration retains local linear convergence, with the convergence rate depending on the accuracy of the inexact computations. Moreover, we prove that the fixed points of the algorithm are precisely critical points of the prescribed index. In addition, we introduce an adaptive strategy for updating the cubic regularization parameter to improve robustness and computational efficiency of the proposed algorithm. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted convergence behavior, demonstrate the effectiveness of the adaptive strategy, and illustrate the capability of the proposed method for locating critical points and constructing solution landscapes.

</details>


### [164] [Computational Trade-Offs Between Newton and Picard Solvers for Mean Field Game PDE Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11050)
*Mathieu Lauriere,Andrew Shi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文比较了均场博弈前向-后向偏微分方程半隐式有限差分离散的两种求解器：外层不动点迭代的Picard法与直接求解耦合非线性时空系统的Newton法，分析其在计算成本、鲁棒性和稀疏结构上的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对同一均场博弈前向-后向偏微分方程离散系统，需要理解Picard与Newton两类求解器在不同粘性、时间冲击及Hamiltonian类型下的计算成本与鲁棒性权衡，以指导求解器选择。

Method: 在一维和二维MFG基准上对比Picard法（交替前向Fokker-Planck与后向Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman求解的外层不动点迭代）与Newton法（直接对耦合非线性时空系统施加Newton法），并结合参数延拓；通过残差、Jacobian块与可分离、局部不可分离、非局部Hamiltonian产生的稀疏结构分析其权衡；在二维双井MFG上测试混合延拓策略，先在低成本粘性下降阶段用Picard再切换至Newton延拓。

Result: Picard法收敛时wall-clock成本显著更低，但在足够低粘性下可能失败，并在时间冲击下需要强阻尼；采用参数延拓时，Newton法在这些情形下更鲁棒，代价是更大的耦合线性系统。

Conclusion: Picard与Newton的权衡可用残差、Jacobian块与稀疏结构解释；混合延拓策略可结合Picard的低成本与Newton的鲁棒性，为MFG前向-后向PDE求解提供实用方案。

Abstract: We study computational trade-offs between two solvers for the same semi-implicit finite-difference discretization of forward-backward partial differential equation (PDE) systems arising in mean field games (MFGs). The Picard method uses an outer fixed-point iteration that alternates a forward Fokker-Planck solve and a backward Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman solve. The Newton method instead applies Newton's method directly to the coupled nonlinear space-time system. Across one- and two-dimensional MFG benchmarks, we find that the Picard method has much lower wall-clock cost when it converges, but may fail at sufficiently low viscosity and may require strong damping under temporal shocks. With parameter continuation, the Newton method is more robust in these regimes, at the cost of larger coupled linear systems. We relate these trade-offs to the residuals, Jacobian blocks, and sparsity structures produced by separable, local nonseparable, and nonlocal Hamiltonians. We also test a hybrid continuation strategy on a two-dimensional double-well MFG benchmark, using Picard iterations for the inexpensive part of the viscosity descent before switching to Newton continuation.

</details>


### [165] [Polynomial preserving recoveries of edge element method on Cartesian grids for the time-harmonic Maxwell equations with large wave number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11064)
*Shuaishuai Lu,Haijun Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对三维时谐Maxwell方程，在Cartesian网格上提出PPR恢复算子实现二阶超收敛，并进一步提出CIP-EEM方法以改善相位误差、减轻污染效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低阶Nédélec边缘元在大波数下存在污染效应，且后验恢复技术能否缓解该效应尚不明确。

Method: 构造分别作用于解与其旋度的多项式保持恢复（PPR）算子，并设计含法向跳跃罚项的连续内罚边缘元方法（CIP-EEM）。

Result: 在 κ³h²C_sol 充分小条件下，恢复旋度与恢复解均获得二阶超收敛估计；PPR无法缓解污染效应；合理选取罚参数可使CIP-EEM的相位误差在κh上改善两个数量级。

Conclusion: PPR可提供超收敛但无法消除污染效应，CIP-EEM能有效降低高频区的污染误差。

Abstract: This paper considers the lowest-order first type Nédélec edge element method (EEM) on Cartesian grids for the three-dimensional time-harmonic Maxwell equations with a large wave number. New polynomial preserving recovery (PPR) operators are proposed for the curl of the edge element solution and for the solution itself, respectively. Under the condition that $κ^3 h^2 C_{\mathrm{sol}}$ is sufficiently small, second-order superconvergence estimates are proved for both the recovered curl and the recovered solution, where $κ$ is the wave number, $h$ is the mesh size, and $C_{\mathrm{sol}}$ is a stability constant associated with the Maxwell solution operator. In particular, the analysis shows that the proposed PPR procedures cannot mitigate the well-known pollution effect inherent to the EEM. To reduce the pollution error, we further propose a new continuous interior penalty edge element method (CIP-EEM) that incorporates an additional normal-jump penalty term. It is shown that by appropriately choosing the penalty parameters, the new CIP-EEM can improve the phase error by two orders in $κh$. Numerical experiments are presented to confirm the theoretical superconvergence results and to demonstrate that the CIP-EEM can effectively reduce the pollution error in the high-frequency regime.

</details>


### [166] [Adaptive Diagonally Implicit Runge-Kutta Methods Devoid of Order Reduction for Semilinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11069)
*Steven B. Roberts,Abhijit Biswas,David Shirokoff,Benjamin Seibold*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文基于先前推导的刚性自适应阶条件，构造出首个满足该条件、不出现阶数降低的四阶与五阶DIRK方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性不仅给Runge-Kutta方法带来稳定性挑战，还会降低收敛阶。这种阶数降低现象源于经典收敛分析所依赖的假设（如渐近小步长）在刚性问题中不成立。作者此前已给出对刚性一致成立的锐利阶条件与全局误差界，本文旨在利用这些条件设计不出现阶数降低的高阶DIRK方法。

Method: 将先前针对半线性ODE导出的刚性一致锐利阶条件作为设计约束，构造满足这些条件的四阶与五阶对角隐式Runge-Kutta（DIRK）方法；并借助嵌入方法进行局部误差估计以支持自适应步长控制。

Result: 数值结果表明，在广泛的一类相关非线性测试问题上，新方法能有效缓解阶数降低，通过嵌入准确估计局部误差，并可优于经典DIRK方法。

Conclusion: 本文给出了首个不出现阶数降低的四阶和五阶DIRK方法，验证了基于刚性一致阶条件进行方法构造的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Diagonally implicit Runge-Kutta (DIRK) methods are a prominent class of numerical methods for solving stiff systems of ordinary differential equations (ODEs). Stiffness does not only impose stability challenges on Runge-Kutta methods; it can also degrade the order of convergence. This so-called order reduction phenomenon occurs when assumptions used for classical convergence analysis, e.g., an asymptotically small step size, fail to hold. In a prior paper by the authors, sharp order conditions and global error bounds for Runge-Kutta methods were developed, which hold uniformly with respect to stiffness when applied to a wide class of semilinear ODEs. In this work, those conditions are leveraged to construct the first DIRK methods of order four and five which satisfy these conditions and thus do not exhibit order reduction. Numerical results demonstrate that for a broad class of relevant nonlinear test problems, these new methods successfully mitigate order reduction, accurately estimate local error via an embedding for adaptive step size control, and can outperform classical DIRK methods.

</details>


### [167] [Additive reduced basis preconditioners for large-scale parametrized PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11097)
*Xinnan Fan,Qixiao Hu,Shiquan Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一类加性降基预处理子，通过引入缩放单位校正或基于基础预处理子的投影校正来正则化奇异降阶近似，在FGMRES框架中在线构建降基分量，并给出非奇异性条件与误差界，数值实验表明方法收敛快、显著降低在线求解时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散参数化PDE产生的大规模线性系统迭代求解成本高，降阶近似固有奇异，难以直接作为有效的预处理子，需要一种能保持廉价且保证可逆性的预处理构造。

Method: 提出加性降基（ARB）预处理子：对奇异降阶近似加入简单校正项（缩放单位校正或基于基础预处理子的投影校正），得到在易检验条件下非奇异的预处理子；构造与应用嵌入FGMRES框架，采用离线策略在每个FGMRES步通过POD动态构建降基分量。

Result: 建立了预处理子非奇异性的充分条件，推导了预处理Richardson迭代的误差界；针对对流扩散、各向异性涡、Stokes及Helmholtz问题的数值结果验证了ARB预处理子的效率与收敛性，通常在少数迭代内收敛并大幅缩短在线求解时间。

Conclusion: ARB预处理子能有效加速参数化PDE离散系统的迭代求解，兼顾可逆性保障与收敛效率，适用于多查询工程场景。

Abstract: We introduce a class of additive reduced basis preconditioners designed to accelerate the iterative solution of large-scale linear systems arising from discretized parametrized PDEs. The main idea is to regularize the inherently singular reduced-order approximation by adding simple correction terms: either a scaled identity correction or a projected correction based on a basic preconditioner. This yields nonsingular preconditioners under explicit and easily checked conditions. The construction and application of the preconditioners are integrated into an FGMRES framework through an offline strategy that dynamically builds the reduced-basis component by proper orthogonal decomposition at each FGMRES step. We establish sufficient conditions for the nonsingularity of the preconditioners and derive error bounds for the preconditioned Richardson iteration. Numerical results for convection-diffusion, anisotropic vortex, Stokes, and Helmholtz problems are provided to verify the efficiency and convergence of the proposed ARB preconditioners. The method consistently converges in a few iterations and substantially reduces online solve time, supporting its efficiency for multi-query engineering scenarios.

</details>


### [168] [Neumann-Neumann Waveform Relaxation Method for Hyperbolic PDE with Time Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11098)
*Deeksha Tomer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了用于求解双曲型时滞偏微分方程的Neumann-Neumann波形松弛(NNWR)子结构方法，针对非均匀子域划分，通过Fourier分析估计收敛性并借助Laplace变换证明有限步收敛，并用数值算例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲型时滞偏微分方程在众多实际应用中至关重要，但其闭式解通常难以获得，因此需要发展精确高效的数值方法；同时现有子结构波形松弛方法多针对均匀子域划分，非均匀划分情形下的收敛性尚不明确。

Method: 针对非均匀子域划分下的双曲型时滞偏微分方程，实现了一种新的子结构波形松弛方法——Neumann-Neumann波形松弛(NNWR)；首先利用Fourier分析给出收敛性估计，随后通过Laplace变换技术证明有限步收敛。

Result: 通过Fourier分析得到了收敛性估计，并利用Laplace变换证明了方法具有有限步收敛特性；多个测试算例的数值结果对理论分析进行了验证。

Conclusion: NNWR方法可有效求解非均匀子域划分下的双曲型时滞偏微分方程，兼具理论收敛保证与数值可行性。

Abstract: Hyperbolic PDEs with time delay are essential for modeling a wide range of real-world applications. Since closed-form solutions are often not attainable, developing accurate and efficient numerical methods becomes crucial. In this work a novel substructuring waveform relaxation method namely Neumann-Neumann Waveform Relaxation (NNWR) has been implemented for numerically solving hyperbolic delay PDEs in the case of non-uniform subdomain partitioning. The convergence behavior is first analyzed using Fourier analysis to obtain an estimate, followed by a demonstration of finite-step convergence through Laplace transform techniques. Various test cases are presented as numerical illustrations.

</details>


### [169] [Dirichlet-Neumann Waveform Relaxation Method for Hyperbolic PDE with Time Delay in Multiple Subdomains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11120)
*Bankim Chandra Mandal,Deeksha Tomer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文将Dirichlet-Neumann波形松弛（DNWR）方法扩展到多子域框架，用于并行求解含时滞的双曲型偏微分方程，并给出1D/2D收敛性分析与有限步收敛理论，数值实验验证了理论并优于NNWR、OSWR和Classical SWR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含时滞的双曲型偏微分方程是建模波传播与振荡依赖历史状态的重要工具。随着应用物理复杂度提升，亟需高效并行计算技术；但针对时滞PDE的高可扩展并行求解器仍是显著的计算挑战，现有方法存在不足。

Method: 将Dirichlet-Neumann波形松弛（DNWR）方法扩展至多子域框架，专门面向时滞双曲型PDE的并行求解；推进其数学框架，并对一维和二维空间配置进行严格的收敛性分析，同时建立多域DNWR算法的有限步收敛性。

Result: 理论上建立了多域DNWR算法的有限步收敛性；通过全面的数值实验验证了理论保证；并与NNWR、OSWR和Classical SWR等替代区域分解技术进行详细对比，凸显所提方法的优势。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个稳健、高度可并行的求解器，能够高效处理复杂的含时滞双曲型PDE，为时滞双曲问题的并行数值求解提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Hyperbolic partial differential equations (PDEs) with time delay are essential mathematical tools used to model numerous physical systems where wave propagation or oscillations depend heavily on their historical states. As these applications scale in physical complexity, efficient parallel computing techniques become essential; however, developing highly scalable parallel solvers for delayed PDEs remains a significant computational challenge. To address this gap, this study extends the Dirichlet-Neumann Waveform Relaxation (DNWR) method to a multi-subdomain framework explicitly designed for solving time-delayed hyperbolic PDEs in parallel. We advance the underlying mathematical framework and provide a rigorous convergence analysis for both one-dimensional and two-dimensional spatial configurations. A central theoretical contribution of this work is the establishment of finite-step convergence for the multi-domain DNWR algorithm. These theoretical guarantees are firmly corroborated through comprehensive numerical experiments. Furthermore, a detailed comparative analysis against alternative domain decomposition techniques namely NNWR, OSWR, and Classical SWR, highlight the advantages of the proposed method. Finally, this approach provides a robust, highly parallelizable solver capable of efficiently handling complex hyperbolic PDEs with time delay.

</details>


### [170] [Mixed Hybrid High-Order Methods for Sixth-Order Problems: Fully Non-conforming and $C^0$-Conforming Discretizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11167)
*Arbaz Khan,Ajay Kumar,Harpal Singh*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究二维和三维六阶椭圆偏微分方程（简支与Cahn–Hilliard型边界条件），基于Ciarlet–Raviart重构将其转化为二阶与四阶方程的混合系统，提出全非协调HHO方法（多边形网格）与C^0协调HHO–有限元方法（单纯形网格），证明稳定性与最优先验误差估计，并给出可靠后验误差估计，数值实验验证理论收敛率与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六阶椭圆问题在Cahn–Hilliard等应用中具有重要性，但其高阶特性给数值离散带来困难。作者希望借助Ciarlet–Raviart重构，将六阶问题降阶为等价的混合系统，从而在一般多边形网格上设计高效、稳健的离散方法，并同时提供先验与后验误差分析。

Method: 基于Ciarlet–Raviart重构，将六阶椭圆问题改写为涉及二阶和四阶方程的等价混合系统，并在适当假设下建立其适定性。针对该混合形式，提出并分析两种离散框架：（1）适用于一般多边形网格的全非协调hybrid high-order (HHO)方法；（2）适用于单纯形网格的C^0协调HHO–有限元方法。在适当正则性假设下证明稳定性并推导主要变量的最优阶先验误差估计；对全非协调HHO方法进一步推导基于残差的可靠后验误差估计。

Result: 建立了混合系统的适定性；证明了两种离散方法的稳定性及最优阶先验误差估计；为全非协调HHO方法构造了可靠的后验误差估计量；数值实验验证了理论收敛率，并展示了方法在一系列mobility参数下的稳健性。

Conclusion: 所提出的全非协调HHO方法与C^0协调HHO–有限元方法能够有效求解六阶椭圆问题，具备理论上的稳定性、最优收敛性和可靠的后验误差控制，并在数值上表现出对mobility参数的稳健性。

Abstract: We consider a class of sixth-order elliptic partial differential equations in two and three dimensions subject to simply supported and Cahn--Hilliard-type boundary conditions. Based on the Ciarlet--Raviart reformulation, we recast the sixth-order problem as an equivalent mixed system involving second- and fourth-order equations and establish its well-posedness under suitable assumptions. For the resulting mixed formulation, we propose and analyse two discretization frameworks: a fully non-conforming hybrid high-order (HHO) method on general polytopal meshes and a $C^0$-conforming HHO--finite element method on simplicial meshes. We prove stability and derive optimal-order a priori error estimates for the primary variables under appropriate regularity assumptions. For the fully non-conforming HHO method, we further derive a reliable residual-based a posteriori error estimator. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical convergence rates and illustrate the robustness of the proposed methods for a range of mobility parameters.

</details>


### [171] [A note on the convergence analysis of Laguerre approximations for analytic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11259)
*Thomas Caussade,David P. Hewett*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该note指出Wang (Math. Comp. 93, 2024)关于广义Laguerre多项式逼近根指数收敛性的证明不完整，并给出补全思路及一个未证猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wang声称给出了Laguerre逼近解析函数根指数收敛性的首个严格证明，但其证明存在关键漏洞，需要澄清与修正。

Method: 通过论证Wang主要结果的证明在积分号下取极限时缺乏正当性，说明如何借助控制收敛定理补全证明，前提是关于广义Laguerre多项式及其加权Cauchy变换的若干非平凡不等式成立；并据此提出一个相关猜想。

Result: Wang证明的核心步骤依赖于未加证明的积分号下取极限；若所提猜想成立则证明可补全，但该猜想尚未被证明，仅由数值实验显示其合理性。

Conclusion: Wang所宣称的严格性尚有缺口，结论的成立取决于一个待证猜想。

Abstract: In a recent paper (H. Wang, Math. Comp. 93, 2861-2884, 2024), Wang has presented a number of results concerning the convergence of approximations based on generalised Laguerre polynomials, claiming to have provided "the first rigorous proof of root-exponential convergence of Laguerre approximations for analytic functions". In this note we argue that the proofs of the main results of Wang's paper are incomplete, because they rely on taking a limit under an integral sign, and this is not properly justified. We explain how the proofs could be completed using the dominated convergence theorem, provided that certain nontrivial inequalities involving generalised Laguerre polynomials and their weighted Cauchy transforms hold. We make a related conjecture, which, if true, would complete the proofs of Wang's results. This conjecture remains unproven, but appears plausible, based on numerical experiments.

</details>


### [172] [Minimax-Optimal Early Stopping for Continuous-Time SGD via the Discrepancy Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11273)
*Tim Jahn,Loucas Pillaud-Vivien,Adrien Schertzer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究不适定线性逆问题中连续时间SGD模型的早停策略，证明基于偏差原则的后验停止规则在源条件与谱多项界下可达到（对数因子内）极小极大最优误差率，首次给出此类后验停止规则下的收敛率结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有连续时间SGD分析（含方差缩减变体）大多只对停止时间给出界，而未量化在随机停止时刻所达到的误差；同时随机动力学的乘性波动振幅随残差本身缩放，使后验停止规则下的误差分析具有挑战性。

Method: 针对不适定线性逆问题中的连续时间SGD模型，采用基于偏差原则（discrepancy principle）的后验停止规则，即残差降至噪声水平时停止；在初始误差的源条件与经验协方差算子谱的多项式界假设下进行分析。

Result: 证明以高概率成立：在该随机停止时刻的误差在相应源类上达到极小极大率，仅相差一个对数因子。

Conclusion: 偏差原则可作为连续时间SGD的自适应正则化策略，尽管存在随机采样引起的持续波动，仍具（对数因子内）极小极大最优性；这是逆问题中连续时间SGD在后验停止规则下首个收敛率结果。

Abstract: We study early stopping for a continuous-time model of stochastic gradient descent (SGD) in ill-posed linear inverse problems. We consider an a posteriori stopping rule based on the discrepancy principle, which stops the dynamics once the residual reaches the noise level. Unlike deterministic gradient flow, the stochastic dynamics exhibits persistent multiplicative fluctuations whose amplitude scales with the residual itself.
  Under a source condition on the initial error and a polynomial bound on the spectrum of the empirical covariance operator, we prove that, with high probability, the error at this random stopping time achieves the minimax rate over the corresponding source class, up to a logarithmic factor. Our results therefore establish the discrepancy principle as an adaptive regularization strategy for continuous-time SGD that is minimax-optimal up to a logarithmic factor, despite the persistent fluctuations induced by stochastic sampling. To our knowledge, this is the first convergence-rate result for a continuous-time model of stochastic gradient descent in inverse problems under an a posteriori stopping rule: existing analyses, including those of variance-reduced variants, bound the stopping time but do not quantify the error attained there.

</details>


### [173] [Scalable Approximate Selected Inversion Based on Single-Level Incomplete $LDL^T$ Factorization and Spectral Corrections for Large Sparse Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11363)
*Tahamina Akter,Matthias Bollhöfer,Olaf Schenk*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出四种基于不完全 $LDL^T$ (ILDL) 分解的并行数值技术，用于计算大型稀疏对称系统逆矩阵的部分元素，并通过低秩特征向量修正与SPAI精化提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型稀疏对称系统逆矩阵元素的精确计算通常代价高昂，需要兼顾稀疏结构稳定性与元素级精度的高效近似方法。

Method: 基于ILDL分解，提出四种并行技术：SelInv（在因子稀疏模式内恢复逆元素）、NInv（对L逆应用截断Neumann级数近似）、Mix（融合SelInv与NInv）以及Mix-SPAI（在Mix输出上施加SPAI精化）；并采用基于特征向量更新的低秩修正以在保持稀疏模式稳定的同时提高精度。

Result: 在来自科学与工业应用的广泛稀疏矩阵集合上报告了所提数值技术的性能表现。

Conclusion: 所提方法为稀疏对称系统逆元素的计算提供了在精度与效率之间的多种可选方案，低秩修正可作为收紧丢弃容差的替代手段。

Abstract: This article introduces four parallel numerical techniques for computing entries of the inverse of large sparse symmetric systems, all grounded in incomplete $LDL^T$ (ILDL) factorizations: (1) the selected inversion method (SelInv), which applies the $LDL^T$ factorization to recover entries of the matrix inverse within the sparsity pattern of the computed factors; (2) an approximate inversion method based on a truncated Neumann series expansion applied to the inverse of the L factor (NInv), providing an alternative at the cost of reduced accuracy; (3) a Mix approximation that merges the best of both SelInv and NInv; and (4) Mix-SPAI, which applies sparse approximate inverse (SPAI) refinement on the output of the Mix method to improve entry-level accuracy. To further improve accuracy while maintaining a stable sparsity pattern, we additionally employ a low-rank correction based on eigenvector updates, providing an alternative to tightening the drop tolerance. We report the performance of the proposed numerical techniques on a comprehensive collection of sparse matrices from scientific and industrial applications.

</details>


### [174] [The tubal Arnoldi method for tensor function approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11369)
*Fatima Bouyghf,Mohamed El Ghomari,Alaa El Ichi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于张量t-积的Krylov子空间方法，核心是张量-tubal Arnoldi方法，将高维张量问题投影到低维张量Krylov子空间，用于近似三阶张量函数相关量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大规模三阶张量计算中与张量函数相关的量难以高效近似的问题，需要利用张量代数结构设计降维方法。

Method: 引入张量-tubal Arnoldi方法，借助t-积的新型代数性质，将高维张量问题投影至低维张量Krylov子空间，实现高效计算。

Result: 应用于参数依赖的张量函数求值及多维常微分方程求解；数值实验表明该方法相比其他方法具有有效性与准确性。

Conclusion: 基于t-积的Krylov子空间方法可有效处理大规模三阶张量函数近似问题，具有较好的精度与效率。

Abstract: This paper describes Krylov subspace methods based on the tensor t-product for approximating quantities associated with third-order tensor functions. Specifically, it introduces the tensor-tubal Arnoldi method, which projects highdimensional tensor problems onto lower-dimensional tensor Krylov subspaces. The derived method exploits novel algebraic properties of the t-product to address large-scale tensor computations efficiently. Applications include the evaluation of parameter-dependent tensor functions and the solution of multidimensional ordinary differential equations. Numerical experiments illustrate the effectiveness and accuracy of the proposed methods compared with other approaches.

</details>


### [175] [Deep operator learning for efficient sampling from invariant measures of stochastic differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11376)
*Lin Guo,Li Lei,Jingtong Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种摊销神经采样器，将算子学习与流方法结合，把SDE系数函数映射为从参考测度到不变测度的推前分布，实现跨SDE族的快速采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SDE采样（如MCMC）成本高，尤其在混合慢、高维或网格方法不可行的场景下效率受限，亟需一种能跨SDE实例复用、绕开混合时间的采样方法。

Method: 将算子学习与流方法结合，学习从SDE系数函数到推前分布的映射（参考测度→不变测度）；把采样成本转移到初始训练阶段，新实例仅需一次编码器前向与少数ODE求解步；针对高维问题引入拉格朗日轨迹传感器表示系数函数及交叉注意力架构；并从理论上证明框架的表达能力与分辨率不变性。

Result: 在1D与2D SDE族上精度可与MCMC竞争，在慢混合情形下取得显著加速；实现跨传感器数量的迁移；在64维相互作用粒子SDE上给出结果，而传统网格方法在此不可行。

Conclusion: 该摊销神经采样框架通过训练阶段摊销成本，使新SDE实例采样独立于混合时间，兼具理论保证与高维可扩展性。

Abstract: We introduce an amortized neural sampler that combines operator learning with flow methods for sampling. It maps SDE coefficient functions to pushforwards from a reference measure to the invariant measures, enabling efficient sampling across families of stochastic differential equations. Our framework shifts traditional sampling cost to an initial training phase, after which new SDE instances require only one encoder pass and a few ODE solver steps, independent of mixing time. To handle problems in high dimensions, we use Lagrangian trajectory sensors for the coefficient functions and cross attention in the architecture. We also theoretically establish the expressivity and resolution invariance of our framework. Experiments on 1D and 2D SDE families show competitive accuracy with substantial speedups over MCMC in regimes with slow mixing, transfer across sensor counts, and demonstration results on a 64D interacting particle SDE where traditional grid approaches are infeasible.

</details>


### [176] [PH2T-splines, Part I: A Reasonable Mesh Assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11443)
*Bingru Huang,Yue Xi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文是三部分系列论文的第一篇，研究层次T网格上最高阶光滑多项式样条（$PH^2T$-样条）的构造，聚焦于分层细化条件下样条空间维数的稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为后续$PH^2T$-样条基函数构造奠定基础，需要确定合适的网格细化条件，核心要求是底层样条空间具备维数稳定性。作者希望找到一种合理的网格假设，使得基构造在分层细化下可行。

Method: 针对双次$(d,d)$样条，研究两类细化条件：一是无限制的层次细化，二是排除可消失T $l$-边的细化。通过构造两组实例，分析各类细化下维数的变化；并引入"模板平移"（template translations）将每个细化区域表示为固定模板在单元索引网格中平移的并集。

Result: 第一类细化允许在不增加自由度的情况下引入新边；第二类细化在每一层都排除可消失T $l$-边并增加维数。但两组实例中维数均不稳定。结合已知的$(d-1)\times(d-1)$模板细化下的稳定性结果，这些实例为后续构造提供了依据。

Conclusion: $(d-1)\times(d-1)$模板细化条件被验证为后续$PH^2T$-样条构造的合理网格假设，尽管无限制细化和排除可消失T $l$-边的细化都导致维数不稳定。

Abstract: This paper is the first in a three-part series on the construction of polynomial splines with the highest order of smoothness over hierarchical T-meshes, referred to as $\PHtwoT$-splines. For splines of bi-degree $(d,d)$, we study suitable refinement conditions for the subsequent basis construction, which requires dimensional stability of the underlying spline space. We present two groups of examples, considering unrestricted hierarchical refinement and refinement without vanishable T $l$-edges, respectively. The first setting permits new edges without additional degrees of freedom. In the second group, every refinement level excludes vanishable T $l$-edges and increases the dimension. Nevertheless, the dimension is unstable in both groups. We then introduce template translations to describe each refined region as a union of translates of a fixed template in the cell-index grid. Together with the known stability result under $(d-1)\times(d-1)$ template refinement, these examples justify this condition as a reasonable mesh assumption for the subsequent $\PHtwoT$-spline construction.

</details>


### [177] [Space-time finite element interpolation of nonsmooth functions satisfying boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11496)
*Thomas Führer,Gregor Gantner,Roberto González,Michael Karkulik*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 构造了抛物问题张量积网格上的Scott-Zhang型时空拟插值算子，并将其用于时空一阶系统最小二乘有限元，建立处理非齐次Dirichlet边界条件的拟最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抛物型方程在时空张量积有限元框架下处理非齐次Dirichlet边界条件时，需要能够保持离散边界条件的稳定投影算子，以支撑拟最优误差分析。

Method: 在张量积网格上构造Scott-Zhang型时空拟插值算子：从经典抛物能量空间到时空张量积有限元空间的线性、一致有界投影，并保持侧向时空边界上的离散Dirichlet边界条件；随后将其应用于抛物方程的时空一阶系统最小二乘有限元。

Result: 所得算子是线性且一致有界的投影，并保持离散Dirichlet边界条件；在此基础上为时空一阶系统最小二乘有限元建立了针对非齐次Dirichlet边界条件的拟最优方法。

Conclusion: 该时空拟插值算子为抛物问题的时空有限元提供了一种保持边界条件的稳定投影工具，可用于非齐次Dirichlet边界条件下的拟最优误差分析。

Abstract: We construct Scott-Zhang-type space-time quasi-interpolation operators on tensor- product meshes for parabolic settings. Specifically, these are linear and uniformly bounded projections from the classical parabolic energy space onto a space-time tensor-product finite element space that preserve discrete Dirichlet boundary conditions on the lateral space-time boundary. We apply our operator in the context of space-time first-order system least-squares finite elements for parabolic equations to establish a quasi-optimal method for inhomogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions.

</details>


### [178] [Approximate Inversion of Discrete Fourier Integral Operators via Hierarchically Semiseparable Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11520)
*Yingzhou Li,Jingyu Liu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种离散傅里叶积分算子(FIO)逆的近似方法,分离线与在线两阶段:离线用蝶形分解(BF)与分层半可分(HSS)近似构造 $K^{-1}\approx \tilde{F}\tilde{K}^{*}$,在线实现快速求逆应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散FIO的矩阵表示稠密且规模大,直接求逆复杂度高。需要一种能快速逼近其逆、并兼具直接求解器与预条件子功能的高效算法。

Method: 离线阶段:(1)对 $N\times N$ 矩阵 $K$ 构造蝶形分解 $\tilde{K}$,支持快速前向矩阵-向量乘;(2)利用 $\tilde{K}$ 与 $\tilde{K}^{*}$ 对随机矩阵的快速作用,构造 $G=K^{*}K$ 的 HSS 近似 $\tilde{G}$;(3)对 $\tilde{G}$ 做 ULV 分解得到 $\tilde{F}\approx G^{-1}$;组合得 $K^{-1}\approx \tilde{F}\tilde{K}^{*}$。在线阶段用该近似快速计算 $K^{-1}u$。

Result: 离线复杂度:一维为 $O(N\log^{2}N)$,二维为 $O(N^{1.5}\log N)$;在线复杂度:一维与二维均为 $O(N\log N)$。数值实验验证了算法对一维和二维 FIO 的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法可同时作为直接求解器或迭代法的预条件子,在保证近线性复杂度的同时高效近似 FIO 的逆。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel method for approximating the inverse of discrete Fourier integral operators (FIOs). Given an $N \times N$ matrix representation $K$ of an FIO, the proposed algorithm consists of two stages. In the offline stage, we first construct a butterfly factorization (BF) $\tilde{K}$ of $K$, which enables fast forward matrix-vector multiplication. We then construct a hierarchically semiseparable (HSS) approximation $\tilde{G} \approx G$, where $G = K^{*} K$, using fast applications of $\tilde{K}$ and $\tilde{K}^{*}$ to random matrices. Finally, we apply the ULV factorization to the HSS matrix $\tilde{G}$ to obtain an approximation $\tilde{F} \approx G^{-1}$. Combining these approximations yields an approximate inverse $K^{-1} \approx \tilde{F} \tilde{K}^{*}$. The offline stage has complexity $O(N \log^{2} N)$ for 1D problems and $O(N^{1.5} \log N)$ for 2D problems. In the online stage, the proposed method approximates $K^{-1} u$ for a given input vector $u$ with complexity $O(N \log N)$ for both 1D and 2D problems. The proposed method can be used either as a direct solver or as a preconditioner for iterative methods. Numerical results for 1D and 2D FIOs demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [179] [Mean Square Error Analysis of Stochastic Runge--Kutta Integrators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11528)
*Xuda Ye*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文分析了Yang与Wang提出的两种强1.5阶、弱2阶随机Runge–Kutta积分器在过阻尼Langevin动力学（势函数在有界区域外凸）中的均方误差，证明其对时间平均的误差为O(1/(Nh)+h^4)，并通过数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为过阻尼Langevin动力学中高阶随机Runge–Kutta积分器的采样与时间平均误差提供理论保证，尤其是在势函数仅局部凸（有界区域外凸）的非凸情形下，量化其离散化最优收敛阶。

Method: 将局部误差分解为均值为零项与较小余项，利用离散Poisson方程把各阶矩转化为时间平均误差的界；针对Yang与Wang的两种随机Runge–Kutta积分器（仅用势梯度、无高阶导数），在Wasserstein-1距离下分析其分布收敛性（二阶、对数因子、关于步数一致），并推导对三阶以内有界导数检验函数的均方误差界。

Result: 该积分器的分布以（至多对数因子）二阶收敛到精确解分布，关于步数一致；对三阶以内有界导数检验函数，N步、步长h的均方误差为O(1/(Nh)+h^4)，在离散化意义下达到最优阶。实验在R^2非凸势上测量强/弱阶与采样偏差，并在CIFAR-10扩散模型上对比两种积分器。

Conclusion: Yang与Wang的两种随机Runge–Kutta积分器在非凸过阻尼Langevin动力学中达到时间平均的离散化最优收敛阶，并在扩散模型等高维采样任务中具有实用价值。

Abstract: We analyze the mean square error of stochastic Runge--Kutta integrators for overdamped Langevin dynamics whose potential is convex outside a bounded region. A decomposition splits the local error into a mean-zero term and a smaller remainder, and the discrete Poisson equation turns their moments into a bound on the error of a time average. We carry this out for two stochastic Runge--Kutta integrators proposed by Yang and Wang, both of strong order $\frac32$ and weak order 2, evaluating the gradient of the potential and no higher derivative. We show that their laws approach the law of the exact solution at second order in Wasserstein-1 distance, up to a logarithm, uniformly in the number of steps. For a test function with bounded derivatives up to third order, we prove that the mean square error over $N$ steps with step size $h$ is $\mathcal O ( \frac{1}{Nh} + h^4 )$, which is the optimal order in the discretization. Experiments measure the strong and weak orders and the sampling bias on a nonconvex potential in $\mathbb R^2$, and compare the integrators on a diffusion model of CIFAR-10.

</details>


### [180] [Convergence of finite element approximations for the one-dimensional stochastic Burgers equation with additive trace-class noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11584)
*Binjie Li,Xiaoping Xie,Qin Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 研究一维含迹类噪声黏性随机Burgers方程的有限元逼近，给出P2半离散强/弱误差估计及全离散强时间收敛率，并用数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对黏性随机Burgers方程的有限元逼近，需建立与正则性匹配的最优强误差估计及弱误差估计，并分析全离散格式的收敛性。

Method: 空间上采用P2有限元半离散；全离散格式为空间P2有限元加时间上drift-implicit Euler-Maruyama格式。

Result: 半离散：在L^p([0,T]×Ω;H_D^{α,q})（p,q∈[2,∞)，α∈[-1,0]）得到关于正则性最优的强误差估计，并在L^p(Ω;C([0,T];L^∞))得到几乎正则最优估计；对终端L^q范数矩与时空L^p(0,T;L^q)范数矩导出弱误差估计，弱收敛率（近）为对应强收敛率的两倍。全离散：在τ≤h²条件下，于L^p(Ω;C([0,T];L^∞))的离散类比空间中得到τ^{1/2-ε}阶强时间收敛率。

Conclusion: 数值实验验证了理论收敛率。

Abstract: This paper investigates finite element approximations of the one-dimensional viscous stochastic Burgers equation with additive trace-class noise. For the $P_2$ finite element spatial semi-discretization, we derive strong error estimates that are optimal with respect to regularity in
  \(L^p([0,T] \times Ω;H_{D}^{α,q})\) for \(p,q\in[2,\infty)\) and \(α\in[-1,0]\),
  as well as an almost regularity-optimal estimate in \(L^p(Ω;C([0,T];L^\infty(\mathcal{O})))\).
  Furthermore, we derive weak error estimates for moments of both terminal $L^q$-norms and
  space-time $L^p(0,T;L^q(\mathcal{O}))$-norms, with weak convergence rates (nearly) twice the corresponding strong ones. For the fully discrete scheme,
  which combines the \(P_2\) finite element method in space with a drift-implicit Euler--Maruyama scheme in time, we establish a strong temporal convergence rate of order \(τ^{1/2-\varepsilon}\) in a discrete analogue of \(L^p(Ω;C([0,T];L^\infty(\mathcal{O})))\), under the condition \(τ\leqslant h^2\).
  Numerical experiments are presented to illustrate the theoretical convergence rates.

</details>


### [181] [Some theoretical and practical results on noisy signals recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11765)
*M. Makurin,Yu. Malykhin,K. Ryutin,V. Temlyakov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究含噪稀疏信号的恢复问题，在字典满足RIP型条件或相干性条件时，为OMP及WOMP算法建立Lebesgue型不等式，并将其应用于OFDM无线通信中过采样Fourier字典下的稀疏信道频率响应恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏恢复理论结果多要求字典为Riesz基，难以处理实际应用中常见的冗余（过完备）系统；而无线通信（如OFDM）中的过采样Fourier字典恰恰是高相干、过完备的，因此需要放松假设、建立适用于此类字典的恢复理论。

Method: 在字典满足RIP型条件与相干性条件的前提下，证明OMP及其变体WOMP算法的Lebesgue型不等式，刻画算法恢复误差与系数分布的关系；核心创新是以RIP型性质替代Riesz基假设。随后建立针对高相干字典的稀疏恢复一般框架，并以过采样Fourier字典及稀疏信道频率响应恢复为实例进行分析。

Result: 证明在特定条件下OMP算法能够恢复所有系数较大的字典元素，并给出以信噪比表示的精度估计（采用NMSE度量）。所得Lebesgue型不等式适用于冗余（过完备）系统。

Conclusion: 以RIP型假设替代Riesz基假设，使理论结果覆盖过完备字典；所提框架和Lebesgue型不等式为OFDM等高相干场景下的稀疏信道估计提供了理论保障，OMP在适当条件下可实现大系数元素的可靠恢复并具有可控的NMSE精度。

Abstract: We discuss some theoretical results and their applications to specific practical problems from wireless communications. We assume that we know the noisy version of the signal, which is sparse with respect to a given system of elements (dictionary), at a finite number of points and we want to approximately recover it. This problem of recovery of a noisy signal is closely related to the problem of establishing the Lebesgue-type inequalities for the corresponding algorithms and it motivates us to prove such inequalities. Under certain conditions on a dictionary (RIP-type condition, coherence condition) we obtain different kinds of the Lebesgue-type inequalities for the OMP and its version WOMP algorithms. The most important feature of our new theoretical result is the assumption that the dictionary has the RIP-type property instead of the assumption that it is the Riesz basis, which was used in the previous results. Our approach allows us to treat redundant (overcomplete) systems, which is important in applications.
  We consider the setting of sparse recovery for highly-coherent dictionaries that appear in OFDM setting for wireless communication. Our main example is the oversampled Fourier dictionary and the recovery of the frequency response of a sparse channel. We develop a general approach to such problems and we prove that under certain conditions the OMP algorithm recovers all dictionary elements with large coefficients, and estimate its accuracy (in NMSE metric) in terms of signal-to-noise ratio.

</details>


### [182] [Tolerance-driven close evaluation of the Stokes double layer potential on axisymmetric surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11778)
*David Krantz,Pritpal Matharu,Anna-Karin Tornberg*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种容差驱动的 Stokes 双层位势离面评估工作流：通过快速分类器在标准、升采样与特殊求积之间选择最省算力的方案，并采用稳定化的奇异交换曲面积分处理近表面目标点，在椭球颗粒算例中实现了高精度与低开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光滑轴对称颗粒的 Stokes 迁移率与阻力问题依赖边界积分方法，其核心数值难点在于颗粒表面附近离面点（目标点）上层位势的精确、高效评估。近表面处被积函数近奇异，标准求积精度不足，而普遍使用高成本特殊求积又会带来不必要的计算开销。作者希望构建一种按预设容差自适应选择求积策略的评估框架。

Method: 针对每个目标-颗粒对，先用快速分类器在标准求积、升采样求积和特殊求积中选出满足容差且成本最低的选项。预计算与几何相关的单位密度误差指示量，以约化柱坐标形式列表存储，并在运行时与局部层密度修正因子结合，其开销相对位势评估可忽略。需特殊求积的目标点采用稳定化的奇异交换曲面积分：先用平移奇异交换求积计算周期方位角积分以避免近表面严重抵消，再依据误差指示量对极向做自适应 Gauss–Legendre 求积。该工作流被集成到边界积分求解器中，表面自相互作用使用预计算的 quadrature by expansion 处理。

Result: 在具有挑战性的椭球颗粒构型上进行了数值实验。结果显示目标分类高度准确；几乎所有目标-颗粒交互均达到预设容差，少数未达标案例的误差也仅在容差的适度倍数范围内。该离面框架虽以椭球上的 Stokes 双层位势为例验证，但适用于一般光滑轴对称曲面，并可方便地推广至其他 Stokes 层位势。

Conclusion: 所提出的容差驱动离面评估工作流能兼顾精度与效率，为轴对称颗粒 Stokes 边界积分中的近表面层位势评估提供了通用且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: We consider boundary integral methods for Stokes mobility and resistance problems involving smooth axisymmetric particles. A primary numerical challenge is the accurate and efficient evaluation of layer potentials at off-surface points close to particle surfaces. We present a tolerance-driven workflow for evaluating the Stokes double layer potential at such evaluation points (targets) to prescribed accuracy while avoiding unnecessary computational cost. For each target--particle interaction, a fast classifier selects the least costly option estimated to meet the tolerance among standard, upsampled, and special quadrature. Geometry-dependent unit-density error indicators are precomputed and tabulated in reduced cylindrical coordinates, then combined on the fly with a local layer-density modifier, making its cost negligible relative to evaluating the potential. Targets requiring special quadrature are treated using a stabilized version of singularity swap surface quadrature: the periodic azimuthal integral is evaluated first using translated singularity swap quadrature to prevent severe cancellation near the surface, followed by adaptive Gauss--Legendre quadrature in the polar direction guided by error indicators. We integrate this workflow into a boundary integral solver with precomputed quadrature by expansion for on-surface self-interactions and demonstrate the workflow's performance for challenging configurations of spheroidal particles. Numerical results show that target classification is highly accurate. The prescribed tolerance is met for nearly all target--particle interactions and the error remains within a modest factor of the tolerance in the few remaining cases. Although the experiments focus on the Stokes double layer potential for spheroids, the off-surface framework applies to general smooth axisymmetric surfaces and can be easily adapted to other Stokes layer potentials.

</details>
